### [102] [Doc2FRC: Length-Consistent Document-Level Machine Translation via Fixed-Range Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12674)
*Xiaotian Wang,Youyuan Lin,Zhan Shen,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出固定范围分块（FRC）方法，通过动态规划将文档划分为预设长度区间内的块，并统一应用于训练与推理，缓解长文本机器翻译中的n-gram重复与质量退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM虽可减少文档级机器翻译的输入截断，但直接Doc2Doc翻译仍易出现n-gram重复与质量逐步退化。常见做法是将文档切分为更细粒度块，但传统基于规则的分块无法处理训练与推理之间的长度分布不匹配问题。

Method: 提出固定范围分块（FRC），利用动态规划将文档划分为位于预定义长度区间内的块；训练与推理阶段一致使用FRC，使任意长度输入文档映射到相同的长度分布，从而显著降低训练-测试长度不匹配。围绕FRC设计了轻量级双边界匹配算法用于块对齐，并提出四种训练策略。

Result: 实验表明，基于FRC的微调显著优于直接Doc2Doc微调，在IWSLT2017上超过现有DocMT方法。此外构建了独立于主流DocMT训练来源的10语言测试集GlobVDoc，显示FRC能改善分布外文档翻译。

Conclusion: FRC通过统一训练与推理的分块长度分布，有效缓解长度不匹配，提升长文档机器翻译质量与泛化能力，是一条轻量且有效的改进路径。

Abstract: Advanced large language models (LLMs) with long context windows can substantially reduce input truncation in document-level machine translation (DocMT). However, direct Doc2Doc translation remains prone to n-gram repetition and progressive quality degradation. A common remedy is to segment the document into finer-grained chunks. Nonetheless, conventional rule-based chunking approaches fail to handle the length distribution mismatch between training and inference. To address this, we introduce Fixed-Range Chunking (FRC), utilizing dynamic programming to partition documents into chunks within a predefined length interval. By consistently applying FRC during training and inference, the input documents of any length are mapped to the same length distribution, substantially reducing train-test length mismatch. Centered on FRC, we propose a lightweight dual-boundary matching algorithm for chunk alignment, alongside four distinct training strategies. Experimental results show that FRC-based fine-tuning substantially improves 7B LLMs over direct Doc2Doc fine-tuning and outperforms existing DocMT methods on IWSLT2017. We further construct GlobVDoc, a 10-language test set independent of mainstream DocMT training sources, and show that FRC improves out-of-distribution document translation.

</details>


### [103] [Cognition on Graph: Navigating Massive Knowledge Space via Cognitive Cycles and Bidirectional Graph-Text Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12791)
*Gengxian Zhou,Jian Xu,Zichen Tang,Shiming Xiang,Haihong E,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 CoG，通过"计划-探索-反思"循环实现图与文本的双向协同检索，在七个多跳问答基准上显著超越现有方法并提升探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法在全局异构知识库（大规模知识图谱与文本语料）上进行复杂推理时存在不足：采用反应式、图驱动的探索策略，盲目遵循图拓扑结构，无法根据问题语境和探索进展自适应调整，且图与文本之间缺乏深度双向协同。

Method: 提出 CoG（Cognition on Graph），一种受认知启发的免训练自适应知识探索框架。借鉴人类解题过程，CoG 执行持续的"计划-探索-反思"循环：主动制定调查计划、执行双源（图与文本）检索，并动态反思进展以调整策略。核心在于建立结构化图与非结构化文本之间的深度双向协同——从文本中抽取的实体动态引导图探索，以弥合知识缺口。

Result: 在七个多跳问答基准上的大量实验表明，CoG 显著优于当前最先进方法，同时实现了更优的探索效率。

Conclusion: CoG 通过认知启发的自适应探索与图-文本双向协同，有效提升了异构知识库上的复杂推理能力，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has empowered Large Language Models (LLMs) to tackle knowledge-intensive tasks. However, navigating global, heterogeneous knowledge bases (large-scale knowledge graphs and text corpora) for complex reasoning remains a challenge. Existing methods typically employ reactive, graph-driven exploration strategies, which blindly follow graph topology without adapting to the question context or evolving exploration progress, and lack deep bidirectional synergy between graph and text. To address these limitations, we propose CoG (Cognition on Graph), a cognitive-inspired, training-free framework for adaptive knowledge exploration. Drawing inspiration from human problem-solving, CoG performs a continuous plan-explore-reflect cycle, where it proactively formulates investigation plans, performs dual-source retrieval, and dynamically reflects on progress to adjust strategies. Crucially, it establishes deep bidirectional synergy between structured graph and unstructured text, where entities extracted from text dynamically guide graph exploration to bridge knowledge gaps. Extensive experiments on seven multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that CoG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while achieving superior exploration efficiency. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/zhougengxian/CoG.

</details>


### [104] [DuplexDrama: A Synthesized Dialogue Dataset with Scenarios, Full-Duplex Behaviors, Expressive Speech, and Sound Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12872)
*Qingxiang Guo,Wenke Fan,Shuofeng Zhao,Dawei Yang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Hongwei Cai,Zhou Wang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DuplexDrama，首个同时覆盖完整人设与场景、三类全双工行为、带人设情感标签的表达性语音以及脚本感知音效的合成口语对话数据集，含超 2000 小时音频，并将开源 800 小时中英双语子集以推动全双工口语对话模型研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工口语对话模型研究缺乏同时具备完整人设与场景设定、全双工交互行为（如打断、反馈、未完成话语）、情感表达性语音以及音效信息的数据资源，现有数据集难以支撑该方向训练与评测。

Method: 通过 4 阶段流水线构建 DuplexDrama，同时覆盖四个维度：完整人设与场景设定、三类全双工行为、带人设对齐情感标签的表达性语音、脚本感知音效；对脚本与合成音频进行质量验证，并采用 64 音色池、涵盖 13 种人设与 5 个年龄段。数据质量经内部全双工模型训练验证。

Result: 生成超过 2000 小时音频数据，其中 3.8% 的对话轮次至少包含一种全双工行为；脚本与合成音频质量验证通过，并已通过全双工模型训练验证数据有效性。

Conclusion: 将发布 6400 组双语对话的精选子集（800 小时，中文约 500 小时、英文约 300 小时），以推进全双工口语对话模型研究，并提供示例页面与 LLM 评判评估提示词。

Abstract: We present DuplexDrama, the first synthesized spoken dialogue dataset that simultaneously covers four dimensions: (i) complete persona and scenario settings; (ii) three full-duplex behaviors (interruption, backchannel, incomplete); (iii) expressive speech with persona-aligned emotion labels; and (iv) script-aware sound events. DuplexDrama is built via a 4-stage pipeline; quality validation on both scripts and synthesized audio confirms its quality. We have produced more than 2,000 hours audio data with a 64-voice timbre pool spanning 13 personas and 5 age buckets; 3.8% of all turns carry at least one full-duplex behavior. This data has been validated through internal full-duplex model training. We will release a curated subset of 6,400 bilingual dialogues (800 h, Chinese ~500 h + English ~300 h) to advance full-duplex spoken dialogue model research. Data samples are available at our demo page and LLM-judge evaluation prompts will be released with the dataset.

</details>


### [105] [MedSNIP: Building and Benchmarking Snippet-Level Granularity for Medical Fact Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12884)
*Hasan Iqbal,Sarfraz Ahmad,Hyunjae Kim,Sihyeon Park,Junjie Liao,Qingyu Chen,Preslav Nakov,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出以"片段级（snippet-level）"单位重构医学事实核查，发布人工标注基准 MedSNIP-Bench 与自动片段生成流程 MedSNIP，在保留局部临床结构的同时提升假类 F1 并减少验证调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学声明的正确性往往依赖其周围的临床结构（如检验参考范围、因果或条件关联、患者个体信息）。现有的原子级（atom-level）分解会割裂这些依赖，使验证者面对临床上不完整的声明。

Method: 将医学事实核查重构为片段级验证，采用按从句分组的单元以保留局部临床结构；构建 MedSNIP-Bench（人工标注的片段级医学事实验证基准，含 276 条消费者健康与临床情景回答，切分为 2,524 个片段，具备一般语境与患者语境双标签及六类结构模式编码）；并提出自动片段生成流程 MedSNIP，先以 MedSNIP-Bench 上的人工片段边界进行评估，再用于为外部语料生成片段级单元。

Result: 在 MedSNIP-Bench、HealthFC 和 MedHallu 上，片段级验证保持或提升了假类 F1，增益集中于答案足够长以致碎片化的场景以及验证器足够强、能利用恢复结构的场景；最大的合并模式增益出现在因果—条件临床链上。同时验证器调用次数减少 24–73%，但该节省仅在分解成本足够低时才能在全流程中保持，而开放权重分解器可在不损失分块保真度的前提下实现这一点。

Conclusion: 片段级验证是医学事实核查的更合理粒度：它以保留临床结构的方式组织验证单元，在提升假类识别能力的同时显著降低验证成本，前提是分解环节本身足够廉价。

Abstract: A medical claim's correctness often depends not on the claim alone, but on the clinical structure around it. A claim may require a lab reference range, a causal or conditional link, or patient-specific details to be judged correctly, and atom-level decomposition can fragment these dependencies, leaving the verifier with clinically incomplete claims. We reformulate medical fact-checking around snippet-level verification, where clause-grouped units preserve local clinical structure. We introduce MedSNIP-Bench, a human-annotated benchmark for snippet-level medical fact verification, and MedSNIP, an automatic snippet-generation pipeline. MedSNIP-Bench covers 276 consumer-health and clinical-vignette responses, segmented into 2,524 snippets with dual in-general and in-patient-context labels and six structural pattern codes. MedSNIP is evaluated against human snippet boundaries on MedSNIP-Bench and then used to generate snippet-level units for external corpora. Across MedSNIP-Bench, HealthFC, and MedHallu, snippet-level verification preserves or improves false-class F1, with gains concentrated where answers are long enough to fragment and where the verifier is strong enough to exploit the recovered structure. The largest merge-pattern gain is on causal-conditional clinical chains. It also reduces verifier calls by 24-73%, though the saving survives end-to-end only when decomposition is cheap, which an open-weight decomposer makes possible at no loss of chunking fidelity.

</details>


### [106] [Parameter-Efficient Retrievers for Polish and European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12913)
*Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Michał Perełkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出三阶段训练流水线，用强大嵌入模型与重排器作教师，在无原始相关性标签下训练紧凑高效的多语言稠密检索器（PolDense 17M–1B、EuroDense 435M），实现强质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索系统依赖数十亿参数语言模型，其内存与计算开销使大规模索引、频繁语料更新和低延迟服务成本高昂，亟需在保持竞争力的同时压缩模型规模。

Method: 三阶段训练流水线：跨语言对齐、关系知识蒸馏、对比微调；完全不依赖原始人工相关性标签，仅用强嵌入模型与重排器作为教师生成监督信号；支持长达 8,192 token 的上下文。基于该流水线开发了支持波兰语的 PolDense 家族（17M 至 1B，六款）与支持九种欧洲语言的 EuroDense（435M）。

Result: 在 41 个波兰语任务与 150 个多语言检索任务上评测：PolDense-1B 优于所评测的至多 9B 参数的检索器，PolDense 家族在模型规模上构成帕累托前沿；在 1B 以下模型中，EuroDense 在任务平均与语言平均性能上均排名第一，并在九种语言中的七种领先。

Conclusion: 无需人工标注、借助教师模型监督的三阶段流水线可高效产出紧凑检索器，在显著缩小参数规模的同时保持甚至超越更大模型的效果，全部模型已公开，为低成本部署多语言稠密检索提供可行方案。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems increasingly rely on multi-billion-parameter language models, whose memory and computational requirements make large-scale indexing, frequent corpus updates, and low-latency serving costly. We present a three-stage training pipeline for developing compact and efficient retrievers that remain competitive with substantially larger models. The pipeline combines cross-lingual alignment, relational knowledge distillation, and contrastive fine-tuning. It requires no original ground-truth relevance labels, relying exclusively on supervision generated by strong embedding models and rerankers utilised as teachers. Using this pipeline, we develop PolDense and EuroDense, both supporting contexts of up to 8,192 tokens. PolDense is a family of six Polish retrievers ranging from 17M to 1B parameters. EuroDense is a 435M-parameter retriever supporting nine European languages. We conduct an extensive evaluation covering 41 Polish and 150 multilingual retrieval tasks. The results demonstrate strong quality-efficiency trade-offs. PolDense-1B outperforms the evaluated retrievers with up to 9B parameters, while the PolDense family forms the Pareto frontier across model sizes. Among the evaluated models below 1B parameters, EuroDense ranks first in both task-averaged and language-averaged performance and leads in seven of nine languages. We release all models publicly.

</details>


### [107] [LLM-Enhanced Dual-Branch Learning for Large-Scale Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12915)
*Hui Ye,Jing Zhang,Xiulong Yang,Rajshekhar Sunderraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双分支框架DualMLC，融合自回归解码器与双向编码器的异构表示用于大规模多标签文本分类，取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示的方法多依赖单一主编码器或将辅助特征并入单一排序器，异构语言模型之间的互补性未被充分挖掘。

Method: DualMLC采用双分支架构：同一文档分别经自回归解码器语言模型和双向编码器处理，各分支保持独立表示通路并在共享标签空间上独立估计相关性分数，最后通过晚期logit融合（late logit fusion）合并两个分数向量。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的大规模多标签文本分类基准上达到SOTA；消融实验证实融合异构预测器比任一单分支产生更强的排序效果。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 异构语言模型的表示互补性能有效提升大规模多标签文本分类性能，双分支后期融合是一种有效策略。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-label text classification assigns a small subset of relevant labels to each document from a vocabulary containing thousands or tens of thousands of candidate labels. Although pretrained language models have improved semantic text representations, most representation-based approaches center their prediction pipelines on a primary encoder or combine auxiliary features within a single ranker. The complementarity between heterogeneous language models therefore remains insufficiently explored. We propose DualMLC, a dual-branch framework that processes the same document through an autoregressive decoder-only language model and a bidirectional encoder. Each branch maintains its own representation pathway and independently estimates relevance scores over the shared label space. DualMLC combines the two score vectors through late logit fusion, allowing shared evidence to reinforce relevant labels and branch-specific evidence to compensate for limitations in the other branch's representation. DualMLC achieves state-of-the-art results on three widely used large-scale multi-label text classification benchmarks. Ablation results further confirm that integrating the heterogeneous predictors produces stronger rankings than either branch alone. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/huiyegit/DualMLC.

</details>


### [108] [Fewer Words, Not Fewer Tokens: Measuring the Sanskrit Tokenization Penalty per Proposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12960)
*Devansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究考察梵语在子词分词下的"信息密度"是否留存：按命题而非按词计算，梵语在实际部署分词器下比英语更贵，但在匹配实验下该优势消失，真正稳定的是字符级长度效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语将格、数、人称、时态融合进词尾，并把小句链成复合词，故每词信息密度高。但这种密度能否在子词分词后保留，是另一回事，且应以"每个意义单位"而非"每个词"来衡量，因此需要系统检验。

Method: 在相同的 FLORES-200 devtest 内容上，用词表规模 200,019 以上的已部署分词器对英语、印地语、梵语（SLP1 转写）做 token 计数比较；再训练梵语侧 BPE，并设置匹配的英语对照组（同一语料英语侧、相同算法与词表），在 32,000、64,000、128,000 三种词表规模下，对 8 组匹配对分别与配对匹配、字节匹配的对照进行统计比较（含 95% 区间）；同时把比值分解为字符长度比与每字符 token 数比。

Result: 同内容下梵语 token 数为英语的 1.774–2.187 倍，仅为印地语 token 数的 1.325–1.353 倍。对部署英语分词器，梵语侧 BPE 每命题成本看似比英语便宜（0.887）；但与匹配英语对照相比该反转消失：32,000 与 64,000 词表下全部 8 组匹配对（各规模对配对匹配与字节匹配两种对照）在散文上均高于 1.0，95% 区间不含 1。词表增大差距缩小：128,000 时 BPE 对在域内为 0.983，域外 1.025、FLORES 上 1.116。比值因子化后，每字符 token 数比全程接近 1，留存的是字符级长度——梵语散文相对英语在 SLP1 下并无优势（1.028），梵语诗体才有优势（0.596）。部署现实结论：散文与 FLORES 上，SLP1 梵语对部署 o200k 英语 pivot，每命题需 1.831–2.899 倍英语 token。代码、结果快照与全部表格已公开。

Conclusion: 梵语在子词分词层面的"密度优势"并非稳健属性，而是取决于与谁比较、用什么词表和什么文类；一旦与匹配的英语对照比较，该优势消失，接近奇偶。真正稳定的量是字符级长度，故应以字符长度与命题为单位评估印度语言的分词成本；就实际部署而言，梵语在当代散文与 FLORES 上确实更贵。

Abstract: Sanskrit fuses case, number, person and tense into word endings and chains clauses into compounds, so it is information-dense per word. Whether that density survives subword tokenization is a separate question, to be asked per unit of meaning rather than per word. On identical FLORES-200 devtest content, Sanskrit costs 1.774-2.187 times the English tokens under deployed tokenizers with vocabularies of 200,019 ids or more, but only 1.325-1.353 times the Hindi tokens. Against a deployed English tokenizer, Sanskrit-trained BPE arms then look cheaper per proposition than English on contemporary prose (0.887). Against a matched English control, the same algorithm and vocabulary trained on the English side of the same corpus, that flip disappears: at 32,000 and 64,000 pieces all 8 matched pairs, each size-matched arm against both a pair-matched and a byte-matched control, sit above 1.0 on prose with 95% intervals excluding it. The gap closes as the vocabulary grows: at 128,000 pieces the BPE pair reads 0.983 in domain while staying above parity out of domain (1.025) and on FLORES (1.116). The ratio factorises into a character-length ratio and a tokens-per-character ratio, the second near 1 throughout: what survives matched tokenization is character-level length, which Sanskrit prose lacks over English in SLP1 (1.028) and Sanskrit verse has (0.596). The robust statement is about deployed practice: on contemporary prose and on FLORES, with the Sanskrit side in SLP1 against the deployed o200k English pivot, Sanskrit costs 1.831-2.899 English tokens per proposition under the tokenizers people actually ship. Code, the results snapshot and every table here are public.

</details>


### [109] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 二元选择真值基准存在表面特征泄漏，模型可借此超越随机水平而不进行真正推理；论文提出检测方法与Audit-Prune清洗机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元选择真值基准要求模型在正确与错误答案间选择，但若两类答案在表面特征上存在系统性差异，模型无需真正推理即可超过随机水平，导致基准评估失真。

Method: 使用六特征逻辑分类器检测TruthfulQA中的表面特征泄漏，并验证其他基准中类似问题；提出Audit-Prune机制，通过移除最强化泄漏的答案对来清洗数据集。

Result: 六特征逻辑分类器在TruthfulQA上能以较高准确率区分正确与错误答案；多个基准均存在类似表面特征伪影；清洗后的TruthfulQA表面特征泄漏降至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 表面特征泄漏是可检测且可被下游分类器利用的，Audit-Prune为数据集发布前清洗提供了通用机制。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [110] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 TAM 基准，用于评估大语言模型在超长程序性任务中的长程推理能力，发现现有模型表现极差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多跳推理基准多为短程任务，只需少量检索或推理步骤，无法反映真实世界中需要遵循数百页、规则相互依赖的手册的长程程序性任务，可能高估了模型推理能力。

Method: 构建 Tasks over Application Manuals（TAM）基准，从 ICD-10-CM 临床编码和美国联邦量刑（犯罪量刑指南，计算犯罪等级）两个领域整理真实任务，配备人工验证标签；每个任务需依据包含数万条规则的权威手册，跨章节执行一系列相互依赖的步骤以得出精确答案；评估了通用提示、检索增强生成、ReAct 式提示以及基于 GPT-5 的 agent-harness 基线。

Result: 最佳精确匹配性能极低：ICD-10-CM 编码仅 1%，量刑任务为 15.5%。

Conclusion: 当前基准可能高估 LLM 推理能力，并忽略了可靠遵循长规则化流程这一关键挑战；TAM 数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [111] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Kraken 语音到语音翻译模型，采用低比特率 token 与独立声码器，改善翻译质量与非语言信息迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语音 LLM 的 S2ST 面临两大难题：预测高比特率语音 token 困难，且依赖说话人身份与韵律严格对齐的 S2ST 训练数据。这限制了模型性能与数据获取。

Method: 采用基于单层向量量化的低比特率 token，训练其重建自监督学习（SSL）特征；引入独立 token 到波形的解码器 Autowave-X，并以源语音为条件以增强非语言信息迁移、放宽数据约束。模型 Kraken 在预训练 LLM（Qwen3-8B）上增加语音特征输入与低比特率 token 输出，后接 Autowave-X 声码器，使用 15 万小时多语言多任务语音数据训练。

Result: 在翻译质量上优于 SeamlessM4T-Large v2 与 Qwen2.5-Omni，同时说话人迁移和韵律迁移能力均有提升。

Conclusion: 低比特率 token 与条件化声码器的结合可有效提升语音 LLM 的 S2ST 表现及非语言信息保留能力。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [112] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESRL，一种面向MoE大模型的强化学习框架，通过显式探索专家路由空间来提升rollout多样性，在多个模型和任务上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的RL后训练主要关注优化稳定性和训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件。但路由决定了稀疏计算路径，影响输出分布，是rollout多样性的潜在额外来源，尚未被充分利用。

Method: 作者通过实证发现扰动专家路由能改变输出、增加rollout多样性（类似提高解码温度），但直接扰动会激活不合适的专家并降低质量。据此提出ESRL：保留高置信度专家作为锚点，将随机路由限制在合理候选池内；根据路由熵自适应调整扰动强度；并记录rollout时的专家路径，在策略优化时重放以缓解路由不匹配。

Result: 在top-K、top-1和共享专家路由的MoE主干上，以及数学、科学、代码任务上，ESRL均取得最佳性能，且无额外采样或计算开销。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，平均Pass@1和Pass@8分别比GRPO提升3.2和4.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过利用MoE特有的路由结构来增强RL训练，ESRL有效提升性能；对专家利用率和训练动态的分析揭示了其收益来源。该工作属于LLM范畴（针对大语言模型的RL后训练）。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [113] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Duplex Cue，用于评估全双工语音智能体在"轮内适应"（说话同时吸收听者贡献）方面的能力，发现现有模型与人类存在明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评测多采用二值判断（继续说话或停止），无法表达人类常用的一种第三种反应：在继续说话的同时融入听者刚提供的内容（如补词、纠正、澄清）。因此需要新的评测框架来衡量这种轮内适应能力。

Method: 提出 Duplex Cue 评测框架，将听者意图（反馈信号、协作、打断）与说话者行为（不变继续、轮内适应、让出话语权）分离，其中适应包含确认与内容修订。基于 300 条人工确认的英语即兴对话线索做单模型案例研究，将人类录音响应与在回放听者音频条件下生成的 PersonaPlex 续接进行对比，保留 208 对有效样本。

Result: 在 66 对协作类样本中，人类说话者 68.2% 出现适应行为，而 PersonaPlex 仅 34.8%；该模型更多表现为不变继续（42.4%）或让出话语权（22.7%）。

Conclusion: 评估自然语音交互不仅需要看智能体是否继续说话，还必须衡量其如何回应听者的贡献；当前模型在轮内适应上明显落后于人类。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [114] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAS（Simple Attention Sparsification），一种门控稀疏注意力机制，通过将选择器的连续分数注入注意力logits，实现用语言建模损失端到端优化上下文排序，在推理、长上下文理解及智能体任务中一致优于可训练稀疏注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练后注意力稀疏化方法多采用轻量选择器打分加硬Top-K选择，硬选择阻断了语言建模损失的梯度，只能通过蒸馏逐层稠密注意力分布来训练选择器。这种方式虽让选择器按稠密注意力权重排序，但该排序与固定注意力预算下上下文单元对预测的实际影响并不直接对齐，可能把有限预算浪费在用处较小的单元上。

Method: 提出SAS门控稀疏注意力机制：训练时将选择器的连续分数注入注意力logits，使损失可通过标准反向传播更新选择器，从而实现端到端优化上下文排序。关键设计包括：将门置于注意力softmax内部的对数形式；使用归一化softmax门，以校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前块之间的关系；保留连续选择器分数，使模型学习相对优先级而非仅硬选择。此外实现了内存高效的Triton kernel，将SAS集成到FlashAttention式计算中以支持长序列训练。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解以及智能体（agentic）任务上，SAS在不同注意力预算下均稳定优于可训练的稀疏注意力基线，在紧预算条件下提升尤为显著，表明其能为下游任务实现更有效的上下文排序。

Conclusion: 通过让选择器与语言建模损失端到端联合优化，SAS解决了硬Top-K选择带来的排序错位问题，在多种任务与预算设置下验证了其上下文排序的有效性，为训练后注意力稀疏化提供了简单且实用的方案。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑"结构泛化比词汇泛化更难"是Transformer固有缺陷的观点，认为难度差异源自数据集类型多样性差异，并通过构建新数据集验证类型多样性与两类泛化难度同等相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现Transformer在组合泛化中，结构泛化比词汇泛化更难，并常被视为模型固有限制。本文认为这种差异并非Transformer固有属性，而是源于所用数据集中词汇类型多样性高、结构类型多样性低这一数据特性，因此需要控制类型多样性来重新检验该结论。

Method: 作者定义"类型多样性"为某一类型中不同构造子（constructor）的数量，而非具体词组合数量。在既有数据集（COGS、SLOG）基础上，使用Grammatical Framework构建具有语言学多样性的变体，系统改变词汇类型与结构类型的类型多样性，并观察其对组合泛化表现的影响；同时进一步分析其他数据集属性（如非新测试结构以外的类型多样性、逻辑语义格式的表面性质）的作用。

Result: 实验发现类型多样性与组合泛化的相关性在词汇测试用例和结构测试用例中表现相当，支持了作者的假设，即先前观察到的难度差异可由类型多样性差异解释，而非Transformer本身的结构性缺陷。此外，结果与"复合散度（compound divergence）解释组合泛化难度"的先前命题存在矛盾，并显示其他数据集属性也会影响泛化表现。

Conclusion: 结构泛化更难并非Transformer的固有特性，而更多是数据集类型多样性分布造成的假象；控制类型多样性后，词汇与结构泛化随多样性变化呈现一致趋势，提示未来组合泛化研究需重视并报告数据集的类型多样性等属性。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks. We further investigate the effects of other dataset properties on compositional generalisation, such as the diversity of types other than the novel test structure, and surface properties of the logical semantics format.

</details>


### [116] [HypoKG: Evidence-Disciplined Biomedical Hypothesis Generation Beyond Endpoint Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12260)
*Dominic Okonkwo,Adetayo Okunoye,Ismailcem Budak Arpinar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了整合KEGG、Rhea和UniProt的生物化学知识图谱，基于550条连接酶源与罕见病终点的路径，让六个LLM在四种信息条件下生成13,200条假设并用专家五准则量表评分，发现给出源与疾病终点时假设得分最高但证据基础较弱，而给出完整生物路径时假设更符合已知机制关系，称为"证据约束推理"，并通过打乱中间路径步骤验证了模型确实利用了路径结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够生成生物医学假设，但其究竟是从科学证据出发进行推理，还是仅仅生成听起来可信的想法，尚不清楚，因此需要系统性研究LLM假设生成的证据依赖性问题。

Method: 将KEGG、Rhea、UniProt三大生物数据库整合为统一生化知识图谱，构建包含550条从酶源到罕见病终点路径的基准，令六个LLM在四种信息条件下（仅源酶、完整生物路径、仅源与疾病终点等）生成13,200条假设，采用专家制定的五准则1-5分评分量表进行评价，并通过打乱中间路径步骤、固定端点的对照实验验证模型是否真正利用路径结构。

Result: 同时获得源与疾病终点的模型常生成得分最高的假设，表明LLM能凭极少信息产生有吸引力的想法，但其假设与证据的关联性较弱；相比之下，获得完整生物路径的模型生成的假设与已知机制关系更为一致。打乱中间路径步骤后证据基础显著下降（delta = -0.793, p < 0.001），证实模型确实在推理中使用了路径结构。

Conclusion: 知识图谱通过两种方式支持假设生成：识别文献中缺失的生物端点对，以及通过其机制路径引导LLM在端点之间进行推理；LLM可表现出"证据约束推理"，但信息越少时假设越具吸引力却越缺乏证据支撑。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate biomedical hypotheses, but it remains unclear whether they truly reason from scientific evidence or simply produce convincing-sounding ideas. To study this, we combine three major biological databases: the Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG), Rhea, and UniProt, into a unified biochemical knowledge graph and construct a benchmark of 550 paths connecting enzyme sources to rare disease endpoints, yielding 13,200 hypotheses from six LLMs under four conditions varying the biological information each model receives: source enzyme only, full biological path, or source and disease endpoint only. Hypotheses are scored using an expert-derived five-criterion rubric on a 1-5 scale per criterion. We find that models given both the source and disease endpoint often produce the highest-scoring hypotheses, showing that LLMs can generate compelling ideas from minimal information. However, these hypotheses are less grounded in the evidence. In contrast, models given the full biological path generate hypotheses more consistent with known mechanistic relationships. We call this evidence-disciplined reasoning. To confirm this effect, we shuffled intermediate path steps while keeping endpoints fixed. Evidence grounding dropped significantly (delta = -0.793, p < 0.001), confirming models genuinely used path structure during reasoning. Our findings show that knowledge graphs support hypothesis generation in two ways: they identify biological endpoint pairs absent from the literature, and their mechanistic paths guide how LLMs reason between them.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Occamy-1.0：基于Qwen3.6-35B-A3B后训练得到的低成本协作智能体模型，在多个协作基准上达到同规模最强并在成本-性能帕累托前沿的"低成本拐点"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作智能体需执行跨多次模型调用的复杂工作流（信息收集、工具使用、编码、文件操作），成本与时延在整段轨迹上累积，其实际价值不仅取决于峰值能力，更取决于能力交付的效率。而日常工作中的许多步骤强调的是状态跟踪、协调、恢复与落实，而非前沿规模的推理能力。

Method: 对已后训练的 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 检查点做进一步训练；构建以执行为基础的数据与环境；在多种 harness 下采集可回放的长时程轨迹；采用分阶段后训练来发展并巩固互补的执行能力。

Result: 在广泛的协作基准套件上，Occamy-1.0 持续位列同规模模型中最强之一，并在若干任务上与更大规模的前沿系统保持竞争力；在给定评测与定价协议下，四个代表性基准的总体表现使其位于观测到的成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。工具调用、编码与指令遵循的辅助评测表明该专精化保留了广泛的智能体能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明面向协作场景的专精化后训练可在低成本下实现有竞争力的智能体能力，并开源模型权重与部分训练数据以支持实用协作智能体与智能体后训练的研究。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [118] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HORIZON：面向Lux AI Season 3的部分可观测博弈场景的分层智能体，融合对称感知、双记忆信念跟踪、遗物图注意力、信息增益探索与对手条件策略混合，用PPO大规模训练后在胜率、适应性与联赛评分上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lux AI Season 3任务存在部分可观测、回合级动态随机化以及五局三胜的对抗结构，要求智能体兼具战术执行与快速适应能力，现有循环或前馈基线难以同时满足短时控制与跨局元推理的需求。

Method: 提出分层智能体HORIZON，将短时程控制与跨局元推理分离；采用对称感知的空间表征、双记忆信念跟踪、以遗物为中心的图注意力、信息增益驱动的探索，以及对手条件化的策略混合；辅以信念与世界模型辅助目标稳定学习；在JAX大规模模拟器中使用PPO训练。

Result: 在比赛胜率、单局胜率、适应增益与联赛评分等指标上，HORIZON相对强循环网络与前馈基线均取得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 通过分层结构、显式信念建模与对手条件化策略，智能体能够推断隐藏游戏参数与对手风格，从而在部分可观测、随机化与多局对抗环境中实现更优的胜率与适应性能。

Abstract: Lux AI Season 3 requires agents to act under partial observability, randomized episode level dynamics, and a best of five match structure that rewards both tactical execution and fast adaptation. We present HORIZON, a hierarchical agent that combines symmetry aware spatial perception, dual memory belief tracking, relic centric graph attention, information gain driven exploration, and an opponent conditioned policy mixture. HORIZON separates short horizon control from cross match meta reasoning, while auxiliary belief and world model objectives stabilize learning. Trained with PPO in a large scale JAX simulator, the resulting agent explicitly infers hidden game parameters and opponent style. Experiments show consistent gains in match win rate, episode win rate, adaptation gain, and league rating over strong recurrent and feed forward baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAMAE是一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练阶段通过共享潜在注意力模块在潜空间中直接交换ECG、超声、胸片和临床变量的信息，基于120万MIMIC-IV住院数据预训练，在多模态住院任务上优于单模态预训练及强基线，且仅测试时单模态可用仍保持增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊断依赖ECG、超声心动图、胸片和临床变量等互补模态的整合，各模态捕捉心脏生理不同但相关的方面。然而多数医学基础模型仍是模态专用的，仅在微调或后训练阶段才组合模态，这丢弃了临床医生自然整合的跨模态证据，也忽略了每个模态内部的结构。

Method: 提出Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE)，一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练中联合学习患者级表示。它不在事后融合模态，而是通过运行于study-view-entity层级之上的共享潜在注意力模块，直接在潜空间中交换信息，从而支持变量观测的聚合，并能优雅处理缺失模态。

Result: 在超过120万MIMIC-IV住院数据上预训练后，LAMAE在多模态住院任务（院内死亡、ICD-10与DRG编码、住院时长）上优于模态专用预训练以及强对比学习和视觉-语言基线，同时在单模态任务上保持竞争力。即使测试时仅有一种模态可用，这些增益依然存在。

Conclusion: 同时建模模态内与模态间结构可产生更鲁棒、更可迁移的表示。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [120] [What Drives Recovery in Agentic Text-to-Cypher? LAST-CQ: An LLM Agent Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12746)
*Ioannis Prokopiou,Athanasios Aidinis,Panagiotis-Christos Kyrmpatsos,Pantelis Vikatos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以五智能体、免训练、执行验证的 Text-to-Cypher 框架 LAST-CQ 为测试平台，通过三个反事实实验（2,471 条实时数据库查询、六个骨干模型、三个厂商规模层级）剖析智能体流水线中收益来源，发现"检测失败并重试"是核心机制，而非反馈质量或采样数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化查询生成的智能体流水线迅速普及，但尚不清楚循环中哪一环节真正带来性能增益；作者希望厘清"修正""反馈质量""采样数量"等因素各自的贡献。

Method: 以 LAST-CQ（五智能体、免训练、执行验证的 Text-to-Cypher 框架）为可插桩测试床，在 2,471 条实时数据库查询、六个骨干模型（覆盖三个厂商规模层级）上运行三种反事实实验：移除修正环节、用原始数据库错误串替代模式感知的 LLM 合成反馈、将同等调用预算用于并行采样；并校准 LLM 评判器与盲标人类标签。

Result: 移除修正相对单遍系统损失 3.1% 聚合执行 BLEU，相对无精炼反事实损失 12.3%（最弱骨干最高达 80.7%）；用原始数据库错误串替代 LLM 合成反馈几乎无成本（20.9% vs. 19.9% 朴素精确匹配，端到端 <0.2%，双侧检验在 ±0.075 set-F1 内等价）；同等预算用于并行采样反而使质量下降 10–11%。LAST-CQ 自身可挽回 91.7% 单遍失败的查询，且首次成功的查询仍只消耗一次 LLM 调用。序列化结果上的 n-gram 重叠并非双向界：在 65.9% 结果上相对集合等价高估，相对判定语义则低估；LLM 评判器比盲标人类标签乐观 9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 智能体流水线的收益关键在于"检测失败并路由至重试"，而非反馈的精细程度或采样数量；同时提示常用 n-gram 重叠指标存在双向偏差，LLM 评判器需与人类标注校准。

Abstract: Agentic pipelines for structured-query generation are rapidly expanding, but it is unclear which part of the loop produces the gain. We use LAST-CQ -- a five-agent, training-free, execution-grounded Text-to-Cypher framework -- as an instrumented testbed, running three counterfactuals over 2,471 live-database queries and six backbones spanning three vendor scale tiers. Removing correction is worth between 3.1% aggregate execution-BLEU against the single-pass system and 12.3% against a no-refinement counterfactual (up to 80.7% for the weakest backbone). Replacing schema-grounded, LLM-synthesised feedback with raw database error strings costs almost nothing (20.9% vs. 19.9% naive exact match; <0.2% end-to-end; equivalent within $\pm 0.075$ set-F1 by two one-sided tests). Spending the same call budget on parallel sampling degrades quality by 10-11%. What works is detecting failure and routing it to a retry, not the feedback sophistication or number of samples. LAST-CQ itself recovers 91.7% of queries that fail under single-pass generation, while a query that succeeds first time still costs exactly one LLM call. We also show that n-gram overlap on serialised results is not a bound in either direction: it over-scores against set equivalence on 65.9% of results while under-scoring against judged semantics. Finally, we calibrate our LLM judge against blind human labels and find it optimistic by 9 points.

</details>


### [121] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文质疑"LLM进步会带动重大定量决策进步"的假设，指出语言描述是有损编码，并据此提出一个新的模型类别——大规模定量模型（LQM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用机器学习领域普遍假设：定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊、网络入侵遏制等重大定量决策的进步将来自LLM的进步。作者认为这一假设存在根本缺陷，需要从模型所训练的"表示"本身而非模型容量来重新审视。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为训练数据表示的一种性质：语言模型训练于"人类描述所生成的世界表示"，而描述是对定量记录的有损编码，且损失不可逆——任何下游模型无论规模多大，都无法从描述中恢复其未编码的信息。在此基础上，作者进一步指出重大决策场景要求模型具备三项语言基底在构造上无法提供的性质：可复现性、从每个输出回溯到产生它的源记录的谱系、以及经过校准的不确定性。

Result: 作者论证语言基底在构造上无法满足上述三项要求，因此这些性质共同定义了一个区别于LLM的模型类别。

Conclusion: 作者提出并命名了一类新的模型——大规模定量模型（Large Quantitative Model, LQM），主张重大定量决策的进步不应寄望于LLM，而需要一个以定量记录为表示基础的独立模型类别。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [122] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DU-NO多尺度U型谱神经算子，以3.64M参数在近岸波浪预测中较U-FNO提升14.9%且参数少10.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位解析波浪模型（如FUNWAVE-TVD）精度高但代价昂贵，难以支撑集合预报、不确定性量化和实时预警；而现有高精度神经算子在波主导场（如U-FNO）参数量达数千万，成本仍偏高，亟需兼顾精度与效率的算子。

Method: 提出DU-NO（双U型神经算子），一种多尺度U型谱算子，仅在编码器和解码器最浅两层附加轻量卷积U-Net分支；依据采样论证，高频内容只存在于细网格，故全频带局部通路置于浅层，粗层级保持纯谱。采用深度衰减模态调度，将参数量控制在3.64M。

Result: 在公开FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO在六个同训练架构中取得最佳自回归滚动误差，较U-FNO提升14.9%且参数少10.8倍；频带分析显示各频带（含高频带）均有增益。参数匹配对照表明增益来自架构本身：在相同3.6M预算下最佳基线仍落后28.6%。在2D Navier-Stokes上匹配最强基线，在PDEBench浅水滚动上明显胜出。

Conclusion: DU-NO以显著更少参数在近岸波浪及更广泛的PDE任务上达到或超越现有神经算子，验证了将卷积局部通路仅置于浅层的架构设计有效性。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [123] [When Successful Knowledge Graph Edits Displace Correct Answers: Rank-Level Locality beyond Parameter Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12116)
*Yi-Cheng Lai,Jerry Wang,Hsin-Ling Hsu,Li-Chu Chi,Ya-Wen Teng,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 知识图谱嵌入(KGE)模型的编辑在提升目标答案时可能挤走原本正确的答案，且仅基于复用编辑参数事实的局部性测试会遗漏这种排序位移效应。作者提出三范围通用的"排序位移审计"，并给出精确保持选定分数的维度与几何条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编辑KGE模型以推广某个期望答案时，会把正确答案挤出返回列表。仅检验复用被编辑参数的事实，会漏掉这种排序层面的副作用，导致对编辑"局部性"的评估不完整。

Method: 提出统一的排序位移审计，覆盖三个范围：(1) 被编辑参数支撑的事实；(2) 目标查询的其他正确答案；(3) 同一关系下其他查询的正确答案。并推导出使更新既改善目标又精确保持选定分数不变的维度与几何条件。在FB15k-237上以DistMult和ComplEx测试，比较直接推广、严格保持、支撑正则化实体编辑、秩截断保持等策略，并跨维度、评分器、排序约定及学习型编辑器做实验。

Result: 直接推广总能把目标移入前10，但仅23.0–23.2%的编辑无损伤；严格保持虽无测得损伤，却只有1.3–1.4%成功；支撑正则化实体编辑的联合成功率最高（36.3–37.7%）；秩截断保持达32.8–34.7%，并将平均被挤出的答案数从约14降至1.2。局部性同时取决于保护范围与编辑机制。

Conclusion: KGE编辑应当同时报告修正成功率以及排序位移的发生率与严重程度，仅报告编辑是否成功是不充分的。

Abstract: Editing a knowledge graph embedding (KGE) model to promote a desired answer can displace correct answers from the returned list. Locality tests based only on facts that reuse the edited parameter can miss this ranking effect. We introduce a common rank-displacement audit at three scopes: facts supported by the edited parameter, other correct answers to the target query, and correct answers across queries with the same relation. We also derive dimensional and geometric conditions for an update to improve the target while exactly preserving selected scores. On FB15k-237 with DistMult and ComplEx, direct promotion always moves the target into the top ten, but does so without damage in only 23.0--23.2\% of edits. Strict preservation causes no measured damage, yet succeeds in only 1.3--1.4\%. Support-regularized entity editing gives the highest joint success, 36.3--37.7\%, while rank-truncated preservation reaches 32.8--34.7\% and reduces the mean number of displaced answers from about 14 to 1.2. Experiments across dimensions, scorers, ranking conventions, and a learned editor show that locality depends on both the protected scope and the editing mechanism. KGE editing should therefore report correction success together with the incidence and severity of rank displacement.

</details>


### [124] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，用于规范ALD和ALE实验与模拟过程的数据描述，以实现数据的可比较与机器可操作复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子层沉积（ALD）和原子层蚀刻（ALE）的实验与模拟文献在材料科学领域报告方式各异，缺乏统一标准，导致难以比较和实现机器可操作的复用。

Method: 使用schema-miner整理数据，并基于schema-miner pro以QUDT为本体基础，构建四个JSON Schema，涵盖材料、工艺条件、配置及测量或预测结果；随后比较其范围、结构与语义基础，并通过ORKG模板演示schema引导的文献抽取与结构化记录发布。

Result: 构建了四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，并展示了其在schema引导的文献抽取和通过ORKG模板发布结构化记录中的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作为ALD和ALE领域提供了标准化的数据描述框架，有助于提升实验与模拟文献数据的可比较性和机器可操作复用性。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [125] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了 draft-verify-revise 多阶段LLM编排中的"指示语移位"（deictic shift）现象，即不同阶段的LLM对"previous"等上下文依赖表达式的指代理解不一致，并通过合成数据集在6个模型、21种推理配置下测试了其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Draft-verify-revise 是扩展推理时计算量的常见LLM编排模式。当上下文在阶段间级联传递时，不同阶段的LLM可能对"previous"这类上下文依赖表达式产生不同解读，导致指示语移位（所指对象发生变化）。作者希望系统性地研究这一现象及其对各模型表现的影响。

Method: 使用包含10个基础示例的合成数据集，每个示例在三种条件下呈现：保持共享组件不变，仅改变是draft阶段的LLM（assistant）还是verify阶段的LLM（grader）正确解析该表达式，以及revise阶段LLM（meta-evaluator）判定正确读法所需的独立推理量。测试了来自3个提供商的6个模型、共21种推理努力配置，采用e-values进行序贯检验，包含主实验和去除grader反馈中错误分类标签的消融实验。另用独立LLM分析meta-evaluator对每个错误裁决所陈述的理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的非加权平均）从低于随机的0.156到接近完美不等。GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156上升到最高推理努力级别的0.942；Gemini 3 Pro在所有级别均保持0.94以上。Gemini 3 Pro在低推理努力下即超过GPT-5.2在xhigh推理努力下的表现，而每次试验成本仅约5%。当meta-evaluator出错时，其倾向于依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。

Conclusion: 实施draft-verify-revise流水线的上下文工程师应警惕指示语移位问题，并在每个阶段明确表达预期所指对象。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [126] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出会议动态预测基准MDFB及对抗模仿学习方法GLARE，用于生成并评估多轮会议延续内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议延续任务需要跟踪议程、说话人角色、参与者意图及长时多方可讨论中的分歧，现有方法缺乏系统性的面向未来的评估基准与训练手段。

Method: 构建MDFB基准，基于2207场真实会议和24794个面向未来的查询；给定转录前缀与活跃问题，模型一次调用生成合理的多轮延续。提出GLARE，将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成：判别器将真实延续排在当前策略样本之上，其得分提供KL正则化的策略奖励，并通过对当前策略负样本重训练使奖励景观随策略演化。评估维度为效用（朝问题的推进）与拟人性（对话流畅性与角色一致性），无需精确复现观测未来。

Result: GLARE在效用上取得平均0.66的人类评估胜率，在拟人性上为0.70，优于SFT与SPIN，但仍低于真实人类延续。MDFB还可作为社会推理竞技场，通过参考辅助判断对比通用模型（含闭源系统）。

Conclusion: 该基准同时支持任务特定学习与基于输出的会议行为评估，展示了在会议动态预测与模型对比方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [127] [WinSyn: An Automated Pipeline for Realistic Enterprise Question-Answering Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12171)
*Amey Varhade,Ananya Sutradhar,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Navin Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种自动化流程，用于生成模拟真实职场场景的企业邮件合成数据集，包含长短问答及基于数据的标准答案，并评估现有智能体基线，发现平均分低于80%，表明企业级深度研究系统仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境对问答智能体提出极高挑战：企业数据复杂，信息分散于不断演变甚至相互冲突的邮件、聊天、文档等载体中。现有基准缺乏真实世界复杂性，多为短答案和不自然的查询，难以反映企业场景的难度。因此需要更贴近真实、高复杂度的评估数据来推动企业级深度研究系统的发展。

Method: 作者构建了一个自动化流水线，生成反映真实职场场景的合成邮件数据集，并配套生成长短形式的问答题及扎根于数据的标准答案。该方法模拟跨越数月的长期企业项目，涉及最多25名扮演不同角色的交互员工，数据着重体现歧义性、信息分散性和自然产生的查询。随后使用最新前沿模型在数据集上评估若干标准智能体基线。

Result: 在各数据集上，所有查询的平均聚合得分均低于80%，说明当前方法在企业场景中仍有显著改进空间。这凸显了真实高复杂度评估数据对企业深度研究系统开发的重要性。

Conclusion: 企业部署仍面临诸多待解决的问题。该工作表明，现有智能体在真实企业环境下的表现不足，强调了构建贴近现实、高复杂度评估数据的必要性，以推动更强的企业级深度研究系统发展。

Abstract: Enterprise settings provide a challenging environment for question-answering agents, which often rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation, Deep Research (DR), and related techniques. Much of this challenge comes from the complexity of enterprise data: information is often spread across evolving and potentially conflict- ing emails, chat messages, documents, and other artifacts. Existing benchmarks typically have limited real-world complexity, short-form responses, and unnatural queries, so they often fail to capture the challenges of enterprise settings. In this work, we introduce an automated pipeline for generating synthetic datasets of emails reflecting realistic workplace scenarios, along with long- and short-form questions and gold answers grounded in the data. Our method simulates long-running enterprise projects spanning several months and involving up to 25 interacting employees across multiple roles. The data emphasizes ambiguity, distributed information, and naturally occurring queries. To validate the pipeline, we evaluate few standard agentic baselines on our datasets using the latest frontier models. We find that aggregate scores averaged over all queries remain below 80% for each dataset, indicating significant room for improvement. These findings suggest that more work remains to be done for enterprise deployment and underscore the importance of realistic, high-complexity evaluation data for developing stronger real-world enterprise DR systems.

</details>


### [128] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Soft-PNet 用原型分布引导的 Metropolis 采样替代手工可微损失，以统一 KL 目标训练神经符号模型，缓解推理捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型仅在最终标签上监督，中间概念不可观测，导致可正确预测标签却恢复错误概念的"推理捷径"。原型网络通过将概念锚定到少量标注样本缓解此问题，但现有方法仍依赖针对每个任务手工设计、任务相关的可微损失，需逐任务重设，通用性差。

Method: 提出 Soft-PNet，移除手工损失：将概念 grounding 重构为在预计算的可行符号解缓存上的 Metropolis 随机游走，由每个概念单个标注锚点构建的原型分布引导；训练使用一个 KL 目标，对齐原型加权的缓存分布与网络的概念预测。目标函数跨任务一致，且在解空间无法枚举时仍适用。

Result: 在稀缺监督下的 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 上，Soft-PNet 在概念层和标签层与损失工程化的原型网络相当，并能恢复软 grounding 基线遗漏的概念，同时无需损失设计且训练时间更短。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 以统一的、任务无关的 KL 目标替代手工可微损失，在保持性能的同时提升了通用性与效率，为神经符号概念学习提供了更简洁的范式。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [129] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GT Bench基准（覆盖24类经典图问题、44种任务-结构设置、10万+样本、4种表示形式），发现LLM在图推理上的准确率高度依赖输入表示；进而提出GTA智能体，通过偏好训练的表示选择器与"计划-分解"框架，显著提升冻结LLM的图算法推理表现并可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多被要求对图等结构化数据进行推理，但其能否可靠地用语言执行多步图算法尚不清楚。现有评测存在三大局限：任务简单、图规模小；评分侧重代码生成而非图上的实际推理；且固定单一输入格式，无法揭示表示形式对性能的影响。

Method: 首先构建GT Bench基准，涵盖24类经典图问题、44种任务-结构设置、超10万个示例，提供自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表、邻接矩阵四种表示形式。在8个LLM上进行评测分析。基于发现提出Graph Theory Agent（GTA）：由偏好训练的表示选择器与plan-and-decompose（计划-分解）脚手架组成，围绕一个冻结的executor LLM工作。

Result: 评测显示准确率与输入表示强相关，最优表示随图的密度、规模和拓扑以及模型本身而变化，这种敏感性在最强推理模型中依然存在但被削弱。GTA将Phi-4在easy split上从53.5%提升至69.1%，在hard split上从33.0%提升至41.5%，优于8种prompting与agent基线，并可无需重训练迁移到GraCoRe和NLGraph。

Conclusion: 输入表示是LLM进行图推理的关键因素，且不存在普适的最优表示；通过表示选择与任务分解的智能体化设计，可在不微调底层LLM的前提下显著提升多步图算法推理性能，并具备良好的跨基准迁移能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: https://github.com/xzx34/GTA. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/gta/.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号框架实现自动约束获取，用Oracle Transformer模拟用户回答，与FastCA引擎交互，降低人工交互成本，从数据中恢复可解释的约束网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束获取方法依赖与人类专家的密集交互，时间和查询成本高，难以规模化。希望减少人工参与，自动从数据中学习用户定义的概念。

Method: 神经符号框架：训练Oracle Transformer模型来模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识；该学习到的oracle与专用约束获取引擎FastCA交互，FastCA将oracle的响应系统性地精炼为可靠、一致且可解释的约束网络。

Result: 实验表明，这种神经符号交互能有效将数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理对齐，在无需先验领域知识的情况下从数据中恢复结构化符号模型，为组合领域的模型构建自动化提供了稳健方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号交互可自动化约束获取，显著降低用户参与，兼具数据驱动识别与符号推理的可解释性。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [131] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出T-GADE，将热力学遗传算法扩展至大语言模型驱动的结构化产物进化，通过产物级多样性评估与费米/玻色型占据机制控制种群多样性，并在在线装箱任务上以约29%的中位训练过剩降低超越EoH基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将进化计算与大语言模型结合时，既要利用LLM的生成能力，又需对种群多样性进行有效控制；现有方法（如EoH）缺乏对LLM输出多样性的系统性调控机制。

Method: 提出T-GADE，以“结构化产物”（如描述加代码）为进化单元，用LLM实现遗传算子、以产物级指标评估多样性，并引入自由能目标统一支持世代式与稳态更新；采用费米型占据（排除重复基因型）与玻色型占据（允许重复），并给出单成员精确移除及恢复EoH零温生存规则的条件。

Result: 在EoH论文的在线装箱任务上，世代式玻色型T-GADE在T=0.003时，20次运行下将中位训练过剩从1.152%降至0.815%，约降29%（Mann-Whitney p=0.042，Cliff's δ=0.378）；在其两个最高排名最终候选间进行验证选择，迁移过剩中位数与EoH相同，为0.496%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，热力学选择机制以及对保留下来的产物进行验证式利用，在结合LLM的进化优化中具有实用价值。分类：LLM。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $δ=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [132] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多情节原型网络（MEPN），通过聚合多个不相交支持情节的原型降低工业故障诊断中极低样本下原型方差，在DeFACTO传感器数据集上显著优于单情节基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断通常只有少量标注故障样本，标准原型网络在极低样本（few-shot）场景下，由于每个类别决策仅依赖小规模支持集，导致类原型不稳定。

Method: 提出MEPN，从多个不相交的支持情节中聚合原型，并以其均值作为最终类别代表，从而在不改变编码器架构的前提下降低原型方差。在DeFACTO传感器数据集上进行五类故障分类实验，向真实工业测量中注入合成的偏置、漂移、尖峰和噪声故障。

Result: 在100次独立运行中，MEPN在单样本设置（K=1，聚合N_agg=10个支持情节）下显著优于单情节基线；在相同的10样本支持预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet在统计上无显著差异，说明性能提升来源于原型累积而非固定预算下的更优学习。

Conclusion: 原型累积（多情节聚合）是MEPN提升低样本故障诊断性能的关键机制，在保持简单架构的同时有效缓解极低样本下原型不稳定的问题。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [133] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出简化运动学模型(KM)及三种混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)，将腕部IMU信号映射为全身质心(COM)加速度，在步态与坐立转换中实现高精度且具鲁棒性的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部IMU广泛用于日常健康监测，但难以反映全身动力学，而全身质心(COM)才是生理参考标准。因此需要一种仅依靠腕部IMU即可估计COM加速度的方法。

Method: 构建基于若干简化假设、可仅凭腕部IMU求解动力学方程的简化运动学模型(KM)；并提出三类混合AI建模方法(HKM-NN)，包括串行学习(ser-)与两种同时学习(sim1-、sim2-)，以结合灰箱与黑箱建模。使用10名健康志愿者的腕部IMU与真实COM数据，覆盖六种步态活动及坐立转换(SS)进行训练与测试。

Result: KM模型误差为步态6.7%–12.5%、SS为5.6%；HKM-NN显著提升，步态误差降至5.3%–9.3%，SS最佳误差3.9%。噪声测试下，sim1-/sim2-在高斯扰动下更鲁棒，而KM在椒盐噪声下相对更稳健。

Conclusion: 验证了从腕部IMU估计COM加速度的可行性，表明将生物力学结构与数据驱动学习相结合，对不完美、含噪测量条件下的可穿戴传感应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [134] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文重新评估了深度扰动学习（DPL）在机器遗忘中的三种角色，发现其核心的"直接删除信号"能力在修正实现错误后失效，效用与预热启动效果亦不稳定，需回归更弱的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将 DPL 定位为机器遗忘方法，但支持较弱角色（效用正则、对抗遗忘预热）的证据被用来支撑最强主张（直接删除信号），缺乏角色分离的严格验证；同时公开实现可能存在正确性问题，因此需在匹配协议与精确种子重训练基线下达成分离检验。

Method: 在匹配协议与精确种子重训练基线下，分别测试 DPL 在三种角色中的表现；审计公开实现，发现两处正确性问题：图像方向在增强、归一化张量上计算却应用于原始图像，标签扰动低于 float32 分辨率导致标签未变。修正图像扰动流程后，在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上进行三次配对种子实验，并补充单个种子的 Tiny ImageNet 检验，同时计入方向计算时间。

Result: 修正图像扰动流程后，DPL 在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上三个配对种子中均未通过直接删除标准；其效用影响在不同种子间符号不一致，计入方向计算时间后劣于简单预热启动基线。单种子的 Tiny ImageNet 检验也未支持 DPL 作为正则项或预热启动；发布代码中的预处理不一致使该处的直接比较结论不确定。

Conclusion: 结果仅限于随机样本删除情形，不能否定影响函数方法在其他删除机制下的潜力。作者发布了角色匹配的评估协议与面向扰动式删除主张的审计清单，以推动更严谨的机器遗忘评测。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [135] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIM 是一个隐私感知的多智能体、多用户记忆框架，可将记忆分类为私有或公共并实施索引级访问控制，配套提出首个多用户记忆基准 MUMBench，在可见性分类上达 96.0% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LLM 仅局限于单个用户会话，无法跨会话学习随时间演化的用户偏好；已有智能体记忆系统多停留在单用户层面，无法在用户之间共享可提升下游响应的公共知识。

Method: 提出 AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory），一个统一、隐私感知的记忆框架，支持多智能体、多用户 LLM 系统持久管理私有与共享记忆。系统动态将信息分类为私有（仅某一用户可访问）或公共（所有用户可访问），并通过索引级访问控制确保私有记忆仅其所有者可检索。同时构建 MUMBench，覆盖四个领域、包含私有与可共享信息的多用户交互数据集。

Result: 在 MUMBench 上进行三次独立运行，AIM 取得 96.0% 的可见性分类准确率、58.8% 的严格操作准确率和 70.5% 的状态感知操作准确率。

Conclusion: AIM 在保护敏感数据的同时实现了有益共享知识的可用性，提升多用户系统的协调性与一致性；MUMBench 是首个面向多用户环境、评估检索、创建、更新和删除等多种记忆操作的公开数据集。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [136] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Affective Agent 三层参考架构，用亚十亿参数语言模型在可穿戴设备本地完成个性化干预推理，无需云端或逐用户重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算已能推断可穿戴设备上的用户状态，但在本地推理"是否、何时、如何"干预仍具挑战，且存在云端依赖与逐用户重训成本问题。

Method: 融合紧凑的亚十亿参数语言模型与生理证据、情境及用户历史；架构分为感知、个性化、推理三层，通过宿主管理结构化记忆演化实现个性化，替代逐用户权重更新；实例化于室内环境质量控制任务。

Result: 在留出的模拟器生成纵向场景（涵盖生理变化、情境、信号质量与干预历史）上评估，记忆驱动个性化与两遍结构化推理提升了干预决策表现。

Conclusion: 将决策层移至设备端，展示了从可穿戴状态推断走向闭环、个性化干预的可行路径。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation.
