### [244] [Where Decoder Cosine Similarity Fails for SAE Feature Flow Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12591)
*Hendrik Droste,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Kathrin Korte,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征转移图谱，研究MLP更新特征与残差状态特征如何共同产生下游残差特征，发现多数强因果效应涉及低相似度的特征组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型通过微调、模型编辑和对齐不断演化，需理解其内部计算机制。SAE可提供可解释特征字典，但状态特征与更新特征如何交互生成下游残差特征尚不清楚，因此以MLP更新为切入进行探究。

Method: 构建三元组转移图谱 s_k + u_j → t_ℓ（残差状态特征与MLP更新特征共同预测目标残差特征），并通过消融解码后的更新特征来因果验证候选三元组。在Pythia-160M L7→L8和Gemma-3-4B L21→L22上进行实验（各20M token）。

Result: Pythia实验中识别出38,125个强消融效应转移，其中88.0%的状态-目标与更新-目标解码器余弦相似度均低于0.7。Gemma实验中仅对排序前30,000个候选三元组做因果验证，53.6%的强效应三元组两项余弦相似度均低于0.7，方向一致但更弱。

Conclusion: 特征流图谱可作为表示更新机制的诊断工具，并为引导模型更新的方法提供依据；未来将跨层、跨模型、跨SAE家族验证更复杂的模式。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted through fine-tuning, model editing, and alignment procedures while retaining previously acquired capabilities. Understanding the internal computations that support these adaptations is therefore becoming increasingly important for continual model evolution. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide interpretable feature dictionaries for residual-stream activations and sublayer outputs, but it remains unclear how state features and update features interact to produce downstream residual features. In this work, we focus on MLP updates as a first test case. We construct a transition atlas of triples $s_k + u_j \rightarrow t_\ell$, where a residual-state feature and an MLP-update feature jointly predict a target residual feature, and validate candidate triples by ablating the decoded update feature. In a 20M-token Pythia-160M $L_7 \rightarrow L_8$ run, we find 38,125 strong ablation-effect transitions, but 88.0% have both state-target and update-target decoder cosine similarity below 0.7. As a preliminary cross-model check, a run of 20M-token Gemma-3-4B $L_{21} \rightarrow L_{22}$ causally validates only the top 30,000 ranked candidate triples by ablating the decoded update feature, and 53.6% of strong-effect triples have both state-target and update-target decoder cosine similarity below 0.7. The Gemma result is directionally consistent with Pythia, but weaker, since update-target cosine recovers many of the strongest Gemma effects and the run is not a full-atlas causal validation. Ultimately, our results suggest that feature flow atlases can serve as diagnostics of representation-update mechanisms and thereby inform tools for steering model updates. Future work will validate more complex patterns across layers, models, and SAE families.

</details>


### [245] [Poisson-Corrector Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12594)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MYULA算法收敛性，证明其不变分布与Moreau平滑目标的W2距离为O(h)+Õ(h^{3/4})，并给出达到ε精度需Õ(ε^{-4/3})次迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析经典Moreau-Yosida未调整Langevin算法（MYULA）在复合目标π∝e^{-f-g}下的收敛速率，其中f强凸且梯度Lipschitz，g为凸且全局Lipschitz。目标是建立更精确的W2误差界与迭代复杂度。

Method: 针对Moreau平滑目标π_λ与MYULA不变分布，在步长条件0<h(L_f+λ^{-1})≤c下，结合离散Poisson校正子、active-trace估计，以及精确-Euler两点曲率的共享噪声界进行证明。

Result: 证明sqrt(m)·W2(π_λ, 不变分布)=O(h)+Õ(h^{3/4})，误差系数仅对λ^{-1}呈对数依赖；结合Moreau近似偏差，得到Õ(ε^{-4/3})次迭代即可使sqrt(m)·W2(μ_N,π)≤ε。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了MYULA在W2意义下的高精度非渐近收敛保证，并给出ε^{-4/3}阶迭代复杂度，改进了相关采样算法的理论分析。

Abstract: We study the classical Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for $π(\,\mathrm{d} x)\propto e^{-f(x)-g(x)}\,\mathrm{d} x$, where $f\in C^2(\mathbb{R}^d)$ is $m$-strongly convex with $L_f$-Lipschitz gradient and $g:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ is convex and globally $G$-Lipschitz. For the Moreau-smoothed target $π_λ$ and the MYULA invariant law $\widehatπ_{λ,h}$, we prove \[
  \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,\widehatπ_{λ,h})
  =O(h)+\widetilde O(h^{3/4}) \] under $0<h(L_f+λ^{-1})\le c$, with only logarithmic dependence on $λ^{-1}$ in the error coefficients. Combining this estimate with the Moreau approximation bias yields $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ iterations to achieve $\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π)\le\varepsilon$, for fixed model parameters and initialization. The proof combines a discrete Poisson corrector with active-trace estimates and a shared-noise bound for the exact--Euler two-point curvature.

</details>


### [246] [SIMS: Scale-Invariant Merit-Function-Based Scalarization for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12599)
*Zebin Chen,Fei Xing,Yang Chen,Hua Liu,Andy HF Chow,Yuhua Qian,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出尺度不变的优值函数标量化方法SIMS，解决多任务学习中不同任务损失尺度差异导致的优化偏置问题，理论证明对数变换是唯一满足尺度不变性的变换，实验达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习（MTL）需要在相互竞争的目标之间进行权衡，常被建模为多目标优化（MOO），并通过标量化将MOO问题转化为单目标问题。但作者实证发现，现有基于优值函数（merit function）的标量化方法对目标间的相对尺度敏感：实际MTL中不同任务的损失常相差若干数量级，优化过程会偏向尺度更大的目标，即便底层Pareto最优解对正比例缩放是不变的。

Method: 提出尺度不变的优值函数标量化方法SIMS（Scale-Invariant Merit-function-based Scalarization）。SIMS采用一种变换诱导的优值函数，将MTL的MOO问题转化为对损失量级不变的单一目标。理论证明：尺度不变性的要求唯一地将该变换确定为对数变换；进一步证明该变换诱导的优值函数保持弱Pareto最优性，并允许构造近似误差可控的光滑替代函数。

Result: 在具有代表性的多任务基准上进行了大量实验，SIMS一致优于现有标量化方法，并达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: SIMS通过对数变换实现尺度不变的标量化，从根本上缓解了MTL中因任务损失尺度差异导致的优化偏置，同时保持弱Pareto最优性并提供可控误差的光滑近似，为多任务优化提供了理论与实证均优的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) requires navigating unavoidable trade-offs among competing objectives. This paradigm is frequently formulated as multi-objective optimization (MOO), where the scalarization is favored to reduce an MOO problem to a single objective. We empirically find that existing merit-function-based scalarization approaches are sensitive to the relative scales of different objectives in practical MTL, where task losses commonly differ by orders of magnitude. The optimization process often favors objectives with larger scales even though the underlying Pareto optimal solutions remains invariant to rescaling (i.e., multiplying an objective by a positive constant). To address this issue, we propose Scale-Invariant Merit-function-based Scalarization (SIMS) for MTL. Specifically, SIMS adopts a transformation-induced merit function to convert the MOO problem of MTL to a single objective that renders optimization invariant to the magnitudes of losses. Theoretically, we prove that the requirement for scale invariance uniquely determines this transformation to be logarithmic. We further show that this general transformation-induced merit function preserves weak Pareto optimality and admits a smooth surrogate with controllable approximation error. Extensive experiments on representative multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that SIMS consistently outperforms existing scalarization methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [247] [Correlation-Guided Fast Machine Unlearning via Hessian Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12620)
*Ayushi Thakur,Ruchir Gupta,Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种高效机器遗忘框架，通过识别相关数据点并采用闭式参数更新，实现82倍加速并保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络与分布式安全系统中机器学习广泛部署，需要选择性移除特定训练数据（如被污染或对抗性数据），GDPR等隐私法规的"被遗忘权"也提出类似要求。但现有近似遗忘方法计算代价过高，需对每个数据点重复进行昂贵的Hessian逆向量计算，在入侵检测、垃圾邮件过滤等需处理多个相关请求的场景中形成瓶颈。

Method: 提出计算高效的遗忘框架：识别训练集中的相关数据点，并应用理论推导得到的闭式参数更新规则；通过Hessian阻尼保证数值稳定性，并给出理论保证。

Result: 相比标准影响函数遗忘实现82倍墙钟加速；精度较最先进基线提升10^-2；在七个数据集-架构组合（含大规模CIFAR-100 + ResNet-50）上展现优越遗忘效果，成员推断攻击成功率为0.660，tug-of-war分数为0.950。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证模型效用与理论保证的同时显著降低遗忘计算成本，适用于真实世界安全系统中的多请求遗忘场景。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of machine learning in network and distributed security systems has created an urgent need for mechanisms that can selectively and efficiently remove the influence of specific training data to eliminate compromised or adversarial data points from production models. Privacy regulations such as GDPR's \emph{right to be forgotten} also pose similar requirements. However, existing approximate unlearning techniques remain computationally prohibitive for deployment in real-world security systems, as they require repeated expensive Hessian-inverse-vector computations for each data point removal, creating a bottleneck when processing multiple related requests in scenarios such as intrusion detection systems, spam filters, and threat intelligence platforms. Thus, we introduce a computationally efficient unlearning framework that identifies correlated data points in the training set and applies a theoretically derived closed-form parameter update rule, achieving an $82\times$ wall-clock speedup over standard influence function unlearning while preserving model utility with a $10^{-2}$ improvement in accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines. Our method establishes theoretical guarantees and ensures numerical stability through Hessian damping. Our evaluation across seven diverse dataset architecture combinations, including large-scale CIFAR-100 with ResNet-50, demonstrates superior forgetting effectiveness, with membership inference attack success rates of 0.660 and tug-of-war scores of 0.950.

</details>


### [248] [Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning for Cortical Sulci Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12627)
*Saeb Tounsi,Joël Chavas,Pietro Gori,Vincent Frouin,Denis Rivière,Jean-François Mangin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何到语义的球面迁移学习框架，用大规模无标注数据预训练球面编码器，再通过拓扑先验注入器融入脑沟深部线语义信息，在极少标注（62例）下显著提升皮层脑沟分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层表面深度学习面临两难：每个半球的60余条脑沟拓扑复杂，需要高容量模型；但专家标注极度稀缺（仅62名受试者），易导致过拟合。标准监督方法在数据稀缺场景下难以泛化，尤其是拓扑歧义较高的可变、小型脑沟。

Method: 提出几何到语义的球面迁移学习框架。首先利用大规模无标注数据（UK Biobank，约3万名受试者），采用局部优化策略预训练球面编码器，仅依赖连续表面特征（曲率与深度）。下游标注任务引入提取的脑沟深部线作为显式语义输入，通过软初始化的拓扑先验注入器，将其整合进预训练主干，以在不引发灾难性遗忘的前提下跨越从纯几何到语义的维度域差距。

Result: 该方法优于从零开始训练的完全监督基线，平均Dice达到0.77。局部分析显示，自监督几何先验在可变脑沟和三级脑沟上带来最大性能提升（最高达14.8%），证明显式学习皮层形状对识别其最稀有部位极为有益。

Conclusion: 结合大规模无标注几何预训练与轻量语义注入，可在极小标注条件下有效解决皮层脑沟分割的过拟合与泛化难题，且几何先验对稀有、可变脑沟的识别增益尤为突出。

Abstract: Deep learning on cortical surfaces faces a dilemma: capturing the complex topology of over 60 nomenclature-dependent sulci per hemisphere requires high-capacity models, yet the extreme scarcity of expert annotations ($N=62$ subjects) inevitably causes overfitting. Standard supervised approaches fail to generalize in this data-scarce regime, particularly for variable and small sulci where topological ambiguity is high. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a Geometric-to-Semantic Spherical Transfer Learning framework.
  First, we leverage massive unlabeled data (UK Biobank, $\approx$30,000 subjects) to pre-train a spherical encoder using a locally-optimized strategy. By relying solely on continuous surface features (curvature and depth), the relevance of this pre-training is confirmed by the model's ability to detect localized and rare topological traits, such as sulcal interruptions. The downstream labeling task, however, introduces extracted sulcal fundi (lines) as an explicit semantic input. To bridge this dimensional domain gap (from purely geometric to semantic) without causing catastrophic forgetting, these anatomical lines are integrated into the pre-trained backbone via a soft-initialized Topological Prior Injector.
  Our experiments demonstrate that this approach outperforms fully supervised baselines trained from scratch, achieving a mean Dice of 0.77. Crucially, a local analysis reveals that the self-supervised geometric priors yield the largest performance gains on variable and tertiary sulci (up to 14.8%), confirming that learning the cortex shape is highly beneficial for identifying its rarest parts.

</details>


### [249] [Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Horizon-Resolved Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12639)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HRX（Horizon-Resolved eXplanation），为时间序列预测的可解释性方法引入"预测步"维度，使每个预测步获得独立的输入重要性图，并证明这种步级依赖是低维的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型解释方法大多只返回单一重要性向量，隐含假设所有预测步依赖相同的过去值。作者指出该假设不成立，不同预测步实际依赖不同的历史值，因此需要按预测步分别解释。

Method: HRX是一个即插即用框架，含三个组件：1）估计器，可在不修改时间序列主干网络的前提下，从任意可微预测器中读出各预测步的重要性图；2）评估协议，通过移除重要性图排序最高的输入、观察单个预测步变化程度来验证"预测步轴"的有效性；3）排序准则，提前判断在某条时间序列上是否有必要解析该轴。此外还揭示步级依赖的低维性——所有步的解释由少数共享图构成，且数量不随预测长度增长。

Result: 在多种主干网络和数据集上的大量实验表明，性能提升来自引入的预测步维度，且该结论对以往解释方法的估计器同样成立。

Conclusion: 时间序列预测解释应区分不同预测步，HRX以简单有效的即插即用方式实现步级解释，并具备低维共享结构，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in explaining time series (TS) models have produced methods that identify which past values a prediction depends on. However, most existing methods return a single importance vector, assuming that every predicted step depends on the same past values. In this paper, we show that this assumption does not hold, as different forecast steps depend on different past values. Motivated by this observation, we propose Horizon-Resolved eXplanation (HRX), which adds a horizon axis to the explanation, so that every forecast step receives its own importance map. HRX is a simple yet effective plug-in framework with three components: 1) an estimator that reads these maps out of any differentiable forecaster without modifying the TS backbone, 2) an evaluation protocol that validates the horizon axis by measuring how much a single forecast step changes when the inputs an importance map ranks highest are removed, and 3) a rank criterion that predicts in advance whether the axis is worth resolving on a given TS. We further show that this step-wise dependence is low-dimensional, as the explanations of all steps are built from a few shared maps whose number does not grow with the forecast length. Extensive experiments across various backbones and datasets show that the improvement comes from the horizon axis and holds for estimators of previous explanation methods. Code is available at https://github.com/seunghan96/HRX.

</details>


### [250] [Distortion of AI Alignment Revisited: RLHF is a Decent Utilitarian Aligner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12651)
*Kazusato Oko,Annie Ulichney,Nika Haghtalab,Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对带奖励裁剪的RLHF的失真进行了细粒度分析，证明指数级退化并非算法固有属性，而是偏好数据分布μ与KL参考策略π_ref之间分布失配的结果，并给出了不同KL正则强度下的紧上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF是让大语言模型对齐人类偏好的标准范式，但在用户偏好异质的多元场景下其有效性受到质疑。Gölz et al. (2025) 指出，当用户偏好异质时，失真（RLHF策略的平均用户效用与最优平均效用之间的乘性差距）会随Bradley-Terry温度参数β指数级增长。本文旨在探究这种指数退化是否是该算法的根本性质。

Method: 作者对带奖励裁剪的RLHF失真进行细粒度理论分析，在KL正则化强度的多个区间内建立失真的紧上界与下界。分析中引入B，作为生成偏好数据的分布μ与KL参考策略π_ref之间对数密度比的上界，用于刻画分布失配程度。

Result: 在代表性区间下，Bradley-Terry模型中的失真为Õ(βB + β)。特别地，当不存在分布失配（即μ = π_ref）时，RLHF可达到O(β)的最优失真（至多相差常数）。这表明指数级退化源于分布失配，而非算法本身。

Conclusion: 要在RLHF中合理地最大化平均效用，应优先使用在策略（on-policy）采样的偏好数据，或在RLHF之前先在接近μ的数据源上进行微调，以避免分布失配导致的效用失真。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard paradigm for aligning large language models with human preferences, its effectiveness in pluralistic settings has been called into question. Notably, recent work by Gölz et al. (2025) demonstrated that the \textit{distortion} -- defined as the multiplicative gap between the average user utility of the RLHF policy and the optimal average utility -- can scale exponentially with the Bradley-Terry temperature parameter $β$ when users have heterogeneous preferences. In this work, we present a fine-grained analysis of the distortion of RLHF with reward clipping and demonstrate that such exponential degradation is not a fundamental property of the algorithm but rather a consequence of distribution mismatch between the distribution generating preference data ($μ$) and the KL reference policy ($π_{\mathrm{ref}}$). To this end, we establish tight upper and lower bounds on the distortion of RLHF across multiple regimes of the KL regularization strength. We show that in a representative regime, under the Bradley-Terry model, the distortion is $\tildeΘ(βB + β)$, where $B$ is an upper bound on the log density ratio between $μ$ and $π_{\mathrm{ref}}$. In particular, when there is no distribution mismatch (i.e., $μ= π_{\mathrm{ref}}$), RLHF achieves the optimal distortion of $O(β)$ up to a constant. Our results suggest that, to reasonably maximize average utility with RLHF, it is preferable to use on-policy sampled preference data or to fine-tune before RLHF on data from a source close to $μ$.

</details>


### [251] [ProactiveBench: Can Streaming Video Models Really Interact Like Humans?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12658)
*Kaixuan Du,Xin Wan,YuKun Wang,Hang Zhang,Meng Cao,Dai Guan,Ming Chen,Ni Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ProactiveBench，用于在无显式响应提示的流式视频场景下评估模型的主动交互能力，发现现有系统存在明显的时序决策缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解评估多为“反应式”，只在选定的时间戳查询模型，无法评估模型应在何时响应；而类人的主动交互要求模型持续监控请求、在目标事件后的合适时间窗口内响应、其余时间保持沉默。

Method: 构建 ProactiveBench，以一秒为间隔的流式输入对模型进行评估且不给出显式响应提示。包含六个子任务，涵盖不同触发歧义程度与时间容忍度：Event Sensitivity 在同一段录像上几何式地结合响应率与沉默率；四个基于时间窗口的子任务区分过早、窗口内与漏响应；Duplicate Counting 惩罚遗漏与重复。

Result: 在评估的六个系统中，有四个出现过早响应多于漏响应的情况，暴露出模型在实现类人交互所需的时序决策方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 主动式视频理解评估能揭示传统反应式评测忽略的时序决策问题，ProactiveBench 为衡量模型何时响应提供了新的评测框架。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to process continuous multimodal input while maintaining temporal context. Existing evaluations are predominantly reactive: they query a model at a selected timestamp and therefore do not assess when it should respond. Proactive interaction instead requires monitoring a standing request, responding within an appropriate interval after the target event, and otherwise remaining silent. We introduce ProactiveBench, which evaluates models at one-second stream intervals without an explicit response cue. Its six subtasks vary trigger ambiguity and timing tolerance. Event Sensitivity geometrically combines response and silence rates on the same recording; four window-based subtasks distinguish early, in-window, and missed responses; and Duplicate Counting penalizes omissions and repetitions. Premature responses outnumber missed responses for four of the six evaluated systems, revealing a substantial gap in the temporal decision-making required for human-like interaction.

</details>


### [252] [SIFPBPNet: A Dual-Path Network for Wearable and Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation via Individualized Steady-state Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12690)
*Shuailong Tang,Xiaoyu Li,Donglin Xie,Wei Chen,Guangpu Zhu,Yelei Li,Yali Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双路径架构SIFPBPNet，通过分离稳态与瞬时特征实现连续无袖带血压监测，MAE达8.57/5.97 mmHg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于PPG的无袖带血压监测受限于人群异质性和"一对多映射"问题，即不同个体的相似波形对应不同血压水平，传统人群模型精度受限。

Method: 设计双路径架构SIFPBPNet：稳态特征路径（SFP）用图注意力网络（GAT）从多日历史PPG轨迹中提取个体特异的长时特征；瞬时特征路径（IFP）从当前PPG片段捕捉短时动态，并通过交叉注意力机制引入稳态先验。

Result: 在大型可穿戴数据集上，收缩压和舒张压MAE分别为8.57和5.97 mmHg，优于现有最优模型；SFP模块集成到多种骨干网络时均带来收缩压MAE 2.8%-13.1%的相对降低。

Conclusion: SFP模块具有强泛化性与即插即用迁移能力，为精准无袖带血压监测提供了潜力。

Abstract: Continuous and cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring using photoplethysmography (PPG) is of great interest for low-cost and personalized cardiovascular health management. However, significant population heterogeneity and the "one-to-many mapping" problem, where similar waveforms across individuals correspond to different BP levels, limit the accuracy of conventional population-based models. To address this challenge, we propose a dual-path architecture termed SIFPBPNet, which separately represents steady-state and instantaneous features, through a Steady-state Feature Path (SFP) and an Instantaneous Feature Path (IFP). The SFP employs a Graph Attention Network (GAT) to extract individual-specific and long-term characteristics from multi-day historical PPG trajectories. In parallel, the IFP captures short-term dynamics from current PPG segments and incorporates the steady-state prior via a cross-attention mechanism. Experiments on a large-scale wearable dataset demonstrate that SIFPBPNet achieves a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 8.57 and 5.97 mmHg for systolic and diastolic BP, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art models. Furthermore, the SFP module consistently improves performance when integrated into various backbone architectures, yielding 2.8-13.1% relative MAE reductions for systolic BP. These results highlight the strong generalizability and plug-and-play transferability of the SFP module, underscoring its great potential for accurate cuffless BP monitoring.

</details>


### [253] [Write on Paper and Get the Online Digital Trace:\newline A New Era for Handwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12702)
*Florent Imbert,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil,Tanja Harbaum,Alexey Serdyuk,Fabian Kress,Tim Hamann,Peter Kampf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 结合传感器数字笔与嵌入式AI，在无外部参考系统下实时重建普通纸上的手写轨迹，实现低成本手写数字化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在普通纸张上手写具有丰富的触觉反馈、零延迟以及有助于信息记忆等优势，但要数字化这种笔迹通常需要专用触控笔和兼容基底（如电容屏、EMR平板或特殊纸张）。目前缺乏一种低成本、易普及且有效的方案来数字化普通纸上的笔迹，核心挑战在于：在没有外部参考系统的情况下，准确追踪笔的运动轨迹，同时允许笔在表面上自由移动。

Method: 提出一种创新方案，将数字笔、先进的人工智能算法与自适应AI技术相结合来重建手写数字轨迹。该方法整合了硬件开发（配备传感器的智能笔）与软件创新（利用嵌入式AI实时优化轨迹重建与处理）。

Result: 摘要中未提供具体实验结果或量化数据，仅说明该工作旨在推进手写轨迹自动重建的技术水平。

Conclusion: 该研究致力于实现普通纸手写与数字轨迹捕捉之间的无缝衔接，推动手写数字化的技术进展。

Abstract: Capturing the digital trace of handwriting usually requires a specific stylus and a compatible substrate, be it a capacitive touchscreen, an ElectroMagnetic Resonance (EMR) tablet as used in Wacom systems or special paper. While writing on regular paper offers rich haptics, no latency and is well known for improving information retention, no low-cost and widely accepted, effective solution exists to digitize such a pen trace. The challenge is to accurately track the pen's trajectory without an external reference system while allowing unrestricted freedom of pen movement across a surface. We propose an innovative solution that combines a digital pen, advanced artificial intelligence algorithms, and adaptive AI techniques to reconstruct the digital trace of handwriting. Our approach integrates hardware development, focusing on a sensor-equipped pen, with software innovations to optimize trajectory reconstruction and processing in real time using an embedded AI. This work aims to advance the state-of-the-art in automated trace reconstruction of handwriting, enabling a seamless connection between traditional handwriting on paper and capturing the trace digitally.

</details>


### [254] [InRTL: Effective Intra-Inter Interaction Learning for Relational Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12712)
*Weichen Li,Ken Zhong,Zheng Wang,Li Pan,Jianhua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 InRTL 统一框架，显式建模关系表内与表间依赖，并提升可扩展性，在24个真实任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系表学习（建模通过主键-外键连接的多表）是重要方向，但缺乏针对该任务的系统性建模框架。

Method: 形式化两类交互：表内交互（同表行间关联）与表间交互（PK-FK 连接表间的行依赖）；用列感知表编码器生成初始行表示，再用 Transformer 自注意力与交叉注意力分别建模表内与表间学习；引入线性化注意力与异构图神经网络简化注意力操作以提升可扩展性。

Result: 在10个数据集、24个真实世界任务上实验，验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: InRTL 为关系表学习提供了统一、可扩展的建模范式，实验证明其有效，代码已开源。

Abstract: Relational table learning has recently emerged as an important research direction for modeling multiple tables connected through primary key-foreign key (PK-FK) relationships. Despite recent advances, a principled modeling framework tailored to this task remains underexplored. In this paper, we propose Intra-Inter Relational Table Learning (InRTL), a unified framework that explicitly models dependencies both within and across relational tables. Specifically, InRTL formalizes two complementary interaction patterns: intra-table interactions, describing associations among rows within the same table, and inter-table interactions, describing dependencies between rows across PK-FK-linked tables. To model these dependencies, we develop a column-aware table encoder to generate initial row representations, followed by Transformer-based self-attention and cross-attention modules for intra-table and inter-table learning, respectively. To further improve scalability, InRTL incorporates linearized attention and heterogeneous graph neural networks to simplify the self-attention and cross-attention operations. Extensive experiments on ten datasets covering 24 real-world tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/W1nterFloW/InRTL.

</details>


### [255] [Physics-Guided Synthetic High-Frequency Ultrasound Generation for Skin Layer Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12735)
*Junkyung ju,Kyungho Yoon,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理引导的合成高频超声（HFUS）生成框架，用仿真数据预训练、真实数据微调，实现皮肤分层分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实HFUS数据集仅标注表皮、SLEB等浅层结构，缺乏真皮、皮下组织、筋膜、肌肉等深层结构的密集标注，数据稀缺限制了自动化皮肤分层分析。

Method: 构建多层声学皮肤体模，赋予各层相关声学属性，利用k-Wave仿真生成成对的合成HFUS图像、密集分层掩膜及仿真元数据；将合成数据用于下游分割预训练，再在真实Mendeley HFUS数据上微调。代码与数据已开源。

Result: 合成预训练+真实微调的性能与仅用真实数据训练相当，并在四种可训练架构中的三种提升了平均Dice/IoU。

Conclusion: 物理引导的合成HFUS图像包含可迁移的解剖与纹理线索，可用于真实域皮肤分层分割，但合成-真实外观差距仍需进一步缩小以获取更大增益。

Abstract: High-frequency ultrasound (HFUS) enables noninvasive visualization of superficial skin structures, but automated skin-layer analysis is limited by the scarcity of densely annotated data. Existing real HFUS datasets commonly provide annotations for superficial targets such as the epidermis and subepidermal low-echogenic band (SLEB), while dense labels for deeper structures such as dermis, subcutaneous tissue, fascia, and muscle are rarely available. We propose a physics-guided synthetic HFUS generation framework for skin layer segmentation. The framework constructs multilayer acoustic skin phantoms, assigns layer dependent acoustic properties, and uses k-Wave simulation to generate paired synthetic HFUS images, dense layer masks, and simulation metadata. To evaluate whether the generated data provide transferable supervision, we use it for downstream segmentation pretraining and fine-tune the models on real Mendeley HFUS data. Synthetic pretraining followed by real fine-tuning achieved real-domain performance comparable to real-only training and improved mean Dice/IoU in three of four evaluated trainable architectures. These results suggest that physics-guided synthetic HFUS images contain transferable anatomical and textural cues for real-domain skin layer segmentation, although further reduction of the synthetic-real appearance gap is needed to enable greater gains. The code and data are available at: https://github.com/Finn-02/synthetic-hfus-skin-layer-segmentation.

</details>


### [256] [Optimizing for the decision not the prediction: an exploration of Smooth Net Benefit as a training objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12752)
*Koen M. F. Gorgels,Lasai Barreñada,Maarten van Smeden,Ben Van Calster,Ewout W. Steyerberg,Wouter A. C. van Amsterdam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微的净获益近似目标σNB，用于将模型训练对齐临床阈值决策效用，在Framingham与44个TabZilla数据集上评估其对逻辑回归、GAM与XGBoost的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策常依赖特定风险阈值，而模型通常以伯努利负对数似然（NLL）训练，目标与阈值相关的临床效用之间存在不一致，因此需要决策导向的训练目标。

Method: 提出Smooth Net Benefit（σNB），即Net Benefit的可微近似，用作训练目标；在逻辑回归、广义可加模型（GAM）和XGBoost（三种Hessian实现）上评估；使用Framingham心血管风险数据集及44个TabZilla数据集，共72个数据集-阈值组合。

Result: Framingham上σNB未稳定提升Net Benefit。TabZilla上逻辑回归平均标准化Net Benefit由NLL的0.5669升至σNB的0.5765（均值差0.0096，95% CI -0.0001~0.0193）；GAM由0.5921降至0.5625（均值差-0.0296，95% CI -0.0721~0.0129）；XGBoost中NLL为0.6745，σNB各实现为0.6723~0.6735。逻辑回归中σNB的增益与XGBoost相对NLL训练逻辑回归的性能优势正相关。

Conclusion: σNB效果依赖具体情境，增益有限且主要集中在逻辑回归，对更灵活模型类别帮助不大；结果不支持将σNB作为NLL的通用替代，但支持在似然训练难以刻画决策相关结构的场景中继续研究决策导向目标。

Abstract: Objective Prediction models are commonly trained using objectives such as Bernoulli negative log-likelihood (NLL), although downstream clinical decisions may depend on specific risk thresholds. We introduce Smooth Net Benefit ($σ$NB), a differentiable approximation of Net Benefit designed to align model training with threshold-specific clinical utility.
  Materials and Methods We evaluated $σ$NB as a training objective for logistic regression, generalized additive models (GAMs), and XGBoost with three Hessian implementations. Experiments used the Framingham cardiovascular risk dataset and 44 TabZilla datasets comprising 72 dataset-threshold combinations.
  Results $σ$NB training did not consistently improve Net Benefit in Framingham. Across the TabZilla benchmark, mean standardized Net Benefit for logistic regression increased from 0.5669 with NLL to 0.5765 with $σ$NB (mean difference 0.0096, 95% CI -0.0001 to 0.0193). For GAMs, mean standardized Net Benefit decreased from 0.5921 to 0.5625 (mean difference -0.0296, 95% CI -0.0721 to 0.0129). For XGBoost, NLL achieved 0.6745 compared with 0.6723--0.6735 across $σ$NB implementations. In logistic regression, $σ$NB gains were positively associated with the performance advantage of XGBoost over NLL-trained logistic regression.
  Discussion The effect of $σ$NB was context dependent, with modest gains concentrated in logistic regression and little benefit for more flexible model classes. This suggests that decision-focused optimization may be most useful when limited model flexibility leaves greater scope for improvement.
  Conclusion Our results do not support $σ$NB as a general replacement for NLL training, but support further investigation of decision-focused objectives in settings where conventional likelihood-based training may not adequately capture decision-relevant structure.

</details>


### [257] [Curriculum-Based Adversarial Heterogeneous Agent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Quad-Copter Landing in Maritime Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12758)
*Allan Minh-Tam Nguyen,Sree Showrya Kotala,Stefan Banioi-Crijman,Kurt Driessens,Rico Möckel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究海上环境中四旋翼无人机与船载机械臂的空中捕获问题，采用异质智能体近端策略优化（HAPPO）结合对抗风智能体，学习鲁棒的协同控制策略，并在分布外海况下展现出更优的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上回收无人机受风湍流和船舶甲板运动影响，传统着陆方式易失效，需要探索替代的控制与学习方法。该场景成为检验控制与学习方法的理想测试案例。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中，使用HAPPO强化学习并结合课程学习与对抗风智能体（HARL-AC）训练协同控制策略；将所得策略与基于课程的域随机化方法及单一海况基准进行对比。

Result: 在海况0/4/5的分布内评估中，HARL-AC与域随机化成功率相当，最高达97.5%。在海况7/8/10的分布外评估中，HARL-AC泛化更好，海况10下中位成功率高出最多16%，坠毁率低至14%。对抗训练策略行为更谨慎，超时率仅增加不足3%。

Conclusion: 对抗训练策略在严重且未见过的海况下可实现更安全、更鲁棒的无人机回收，表明该方法在恶劣条件下的泛化与安全性方面优于域随机化方法。

Abstract: Recovering unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in maritime environments is challenging due to wind turbulence and ship-deck motion, making it a valuable test case for alternative control and learning approaches as conventional landing approaches often become unreliable. We study simulated mid-air capture of quadrotor UAVs by a ship-mounted robotic arm, learning robust cooperative control policies with Heterogeneous-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (HAPPO) Reinforcement Learning. We train with HAPPO using a curriculum and an adversarial wind agent (HARL-AC) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab, and compare the obtained control policies against those generated through curriculum-based domain randomization and a benchmark trained on a single sea state. In-distribution evaluation on sea states $0/4/5$ shows comparable success for HARL-AC and domain randomization of up to $97.5\%$. On out-of-distribution sea states $7/8/10$, HARL-AC generalizes better, achieving up to $16\%$ higher median success rate at sea state 10, and substantially lower crash rates of up to $14\%$ compared to the domain randomization policy. Furthermore, we show that the adversarially trained policy shows more cautious behavior, slightly increasing timeouts by $<3\%$, but yields safer recovery behavior in severe, unseen conditions.

</details>


### [258] [Convergence of Stochastic Gradient Methods under Heavy-Tailed Noise and Hölder Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12785)
*Misbah Uz Zaman,Anirbit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究非凸随机优化在Hölder连续梯度与重尾噪声联合松弛假设下的收敛性，分析了SGD、δ-GClip与G-Clip三种方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机梯度方法的收敛保证通常假设目标函数Lipschitz光滑且梯度噪声方差有限，但这些假设在实际中常被违反。本文旨在同时放松这两个假设，研究更贴近实际的重尾噪声与Hölder光滑条件下的非凸随机优化。

Method: 在目标函数具有$(L,s)$-Hölder连续梯度（$s\in(0,1]$）、梯度噪声仅满足有界$α$阶矩条件（$α\in(1,2]$）的联合松弛假设下，理论分析标准SGD、$\delta$-正则化梯度裁剪（$\delta$-GClip）以及标准梯度裁剪（G-Clip）三种方法的收敛性。

Result: 1）当$α\ge1+s$时，标准SGD以$O(T^{-s/(1+s)})$速率收敛；2）$\delta$-GClip在相同条件下达到$O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})$的平稳性速率；3）G-Clip在$α\ge1+s$时恢复上述速率，而在极重尾情形$α<1+s$下达到$O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})$，这是该情形下任何基于随机梯度方法的首个收敛保证。

Conclusion: 论文将经典非凸SGD收敛理论同时推广到重尾噪声和Hölder光滑情形，并为极重尾噪声区域首次给出基于随机梯度方法的收敛保证。

Abstract: Classical convergence guarantees for stochastic gradient methods typically assume Lipschitz-smooth objectives and finite-variance gradient noise, both frequently violated in practice. In contrast, we study nonconvex stochastic optimization under the joint relaxation of these assumptions: objectives with $(L,s)$-Hölder continuous gradients, $s\in(0,1]$, and gradient noise satisfying only a bounded $α$-th moment condition for $α\in(1,2]$. We establish three convergence results. Firstly, that standard SGD converges at rate $O(T^{-s/(1+s)})$ whenever $α\ge1+s$, extending the classical nonconvex SGD rate to heavy-tailed noise and Hölder smoothness simultaneously. Secondly, we analyze $δ$-regularized gradient clipping ($δ$-GClip), a provable trainer of wide and deep nets, and establish a stationarity rate of $O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(1+s)(2α-1)]})$ under the same condition. Thirdly, we analyze standard gradient clipping (G-Clip) and show that it recovers the above rate for $α\ge1+s$ while in the very heavy-tailed regime $α<1+s$, it has a convergence rate $O(T^{-2s(α-1)/[(α-1)+s(2α-1)]})$ --- the first convergence guarantee in this regime for any stochastic gradient based method.

</details>


### [259] [VertiFuseX: Generalizable Financial Forecasting via Multi-Stream Temporal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12793)
*Aashish Bohra,Vivek Vijay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VertiFuseX：基于LSTM倒数第二层垂直融合的轻量级股价预测框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳、高噪声特性，现有深度学习模型依赖僵化的决策级融合、临时性超参数调优和压缩的末层输出，导致信息损失、过拟合及跨市场泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出混合LSTM架构VertiFuseX，在倒数第二层对LSTM、Bi-LSTM、St-LSTM三个分支的多尺度时序表示进行堆叠与重加权融合，并联一个DNN流，在固定超参数配置下通过反向传播联合优化全部组件，从而保留更丰富的中间层多尺度时序信息。

Result: 在2010-2024年10个全球股指收盘价上，采用严格时序样本外测试（最后365个交易日留出），MAPE降低30-54%，MAE和RMSE提升超40%，在33项指标-数据集对比中优于七个先进模型；消融实验证实倒数第二层融合优于末层融合与决策级集成；显著性分析显示模型稳定关注9-15天中程依赖；极端市场行情下的算法交易模拟显示最大回撤降低、风险调整收益更优；模型仅675k参数、2.6 MB内存、1.5 ms/样本推理延迟。

Conclusion: 倒数第二层垂直融合能有效提升金融时序预测的精度与稳健性，VertiFuseX为金融预测提供了轻量、可解释、可部署的解决方案；该工作属于Other类别，不涉及大语言模型或多模态大模型。

Abstract: Stock price prediction remains challenging due to the non-stationary and noisy nature of financial time series. Existing deep learning models often rely on rigid decision-level fusion, ad hoc hyperparameter tuning, and compressed final-layer outputs, causing information loss, overfitting, and limited cross-market generalization. We propose VertiFuseX, a hybrid LSTM architecture using penultimate-layer vertical fusion of multi-scale temporal representations. VertiFuseX stacks and reweights penultimate features from LSTM, Bi-LSTM, and St-LSTM branches, integrates a parallel DNN stream, and jointly optimizes all components via backpropagation under a fixed hyperparameter configuration. This preserves richer intermediate temporal information across scales. Evaluated on 15 years (2010-2024) of closing prices from 10 global equity indices using strict chronological out-of-sample testing with the final 365 trading days held out, VertiFuseX achieves 30-54% MAPE reductions and over 40% improvements in MAE and RMSE versus LSTM-based baselines, and outperforms seven state-of-the-art models across 33 metric-dataset comparisons. Ablation studies confirm penultimate-layer fusion drives these gains over final-layer fusion and decision-level ensembling. Gradient-based saliency analysis shows consistent emphasis on mid-range dependencies at lags 9-15 days. Economic validation via algorithmic trading simulation under extreme market regimes shows reduced maximum drawdowns and superior risk-adjusted returns. With 675k parameters, a 2.6 MB memory footprint, and 1.5 ms/sample inference latency, VertiFuseX offers a lightweight, interpretable, deployment-ready framework for robust financial forecasting.

</details>


### [260] [GenOR-Twin: A Semantic Middleware for Integrating Operational Discourse with Mathematical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12863)
*Rahimeh Neamatian Monemi,Shahin Gelareh,Lubin Cui,Nelson Maculan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GenOR-Twin神经符号框架，将大语言模型作为"语义翻译器"而非直接求解器，把非结构化运维日志转化为数学优化约束，在六个优化领域实现可泛化的自适应决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化运维日志与严谨数学优化之间存在翻译鸿沟：纯精确组合方法难以处理定性、非结构化的人类输入与突发扰动，而直接用大语言模型求解又缺乏可行性保证。同时，数字孪生需要运维观测与优化决策之间的双向同步。

Method: 构建神经符号框架GenOR-Twin：LLM仅承担语义翻译角色，保证精确组合方法的可行性保障；设计动态约束注入机制，将定性扰动事件在运行时翻译为形式化数学约束，实时改变优化问题的可行域；通过知识图谱实现双向耦合（观测更新虚拟模型状态、优化决策回写图谱）以满足数字孪生同步要求；并引入自适应决策策略，依据系统松弛量在低复杂度调度修复与全局重优化之间自动选择。

Result: 在六个不同的优化领域验证了该方法的泛化能力，表明框架可将静态模型转化为能够适应真实环境运维不确定性与变异性的韧性系统。

Conclusion: 该工作表明将LLM定位为语义翻译器而非求解器，是兼顾非结构化输入处理能力与组合优化可行性保证的有效路径，并为数字孪生中的实时人机协同决策提供了可泛化范式。

Abstract: We introduce GenOR-Twin, a neuro-symbolic framework that bridges the translation gap between unstructured operational logs and rigorous mathematical optimization. Our architecture uniquely positions Large Language Models as semantic translators rather than direct solvers, ensuring that the system retains the feasibility guarantees of exact combinatorial methods. { \color{red}We design a dynamic constraint injection mechanism (the runtime translation of qualitative disruption events into formal mathematical constraints) that allows the system to structurally modify the optimization problem's feasibility region in real-time based on qualitative human inputs. The resulting bidirectional coupling---where operational observations update the virtual model state and optimized decisions are reflected back into the Knowledge Graph---satisfies the synchronization requirement of a proper Digital Twin. The framework features an adaptive decision policy} that automatically selects between low-complexity schedule repair and full re-optimization by analyzing the available system slack. Finally, we demonstrate the generalization of this approach across six distinct optimization domains, {\color{red}turning static models into resilient systems that adapt to the operational uncertainty and variability of real-world environments.}

</details>


### [261] [What an odour descriptor corpus can and cannot measure: valence, attenuation, and the ceiling of the public record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12875)
*Stylianos Kampakis,Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审计了机器嗅觉中四个气味描述符语料库的一致性，发现语料库间存在高度异质性分歧，且仅靠结构特征与描述符标签只能达到人类可靠性的约三分之一，缺失的关键方差来自效价（愉悦度）评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器嗅觉模型通常依赖汇总的公共描述符语料库进行训练，但此前从未检验过：同一描述符词在不同语料库中是否度量相同的事物，以及这些语料库整体能度量的性能上限在哪里。该论文旨在回答这两个基础性问题。

Method: 论文审计了Pyrfume的四个语料库。以分子为条件，使用McNemar检验作为语料库效应的精确条件检验；采用I²衡量异质性、四格相关（tetrachoric）与κ系数比较一致性，并用ETS量表评估差异功能。随后以人类评审小组的可靠性为基准，测试Morgan指纹加完整RDKit描述符块、合并语料库标签、模型容量、编码方式、分子数量与词数等对性能的影响，并考察加入效价（愉悦度）评分的效果。

Result: 语料库间分歧高度异质（I²=80%）且随标注广度非均匀变化（z=17.2），无单一偏移可修复汇总问题；中位四格相关为0.795，而中位κ仅0.413，说明各来源在"哪些分子适合某词"上大体一致，但在"多轻易使用该词"上存在差异；109个可估计效应的描述符中有36个在ETS量表上表现出大的差异功能。Morgan指纹加完整RDKit描述符块仅达可实现性能的32.9%，合并两个语料库的全部标签也仅33.9%，且该差距不随模型容量、编码选择、更多分子或更多词而缩小。缺失的方差是效价：每分子一个愉悦度评分即可达54.6%（在独立的较旧仪器上为57.1%），而从描述符中恢复效价（ρ=0.457）仅得16.6%。五个评审者即超过结构加完整描述符记录，十五至二十个评审者达到饱和。

Conclusion: 描述符语料库不能简单地被汇总使用，因为同一词在不同语料库中的测量含义存在系统性差异，且描述符本身无法捕捉机器嗅觉缺失的关键方差——效价必须被直接测量而非从描述符推断。论文发布了描述符交叉映射表和二十个机器验证定理。

Abstract: Machine olfaction trains on pooled public descriptor corpora, but whether a shared descriptor word measures the same thing across corpora has not been tested, nor has the ceiling of what any of them can measure.
  We audit four corpora from Pyrfume. Conditioning on the molecule makes McNemar's test the exact conditional test of the corpus effect. Corpora disagree heterogeneously across descriptors ($I^2 = 80\%$) and non-uniformly with labelling breadth ($z = 17.2$), so no single offset repairs pooling. Median tetrachoric agreement is 0.795 against median $κ$ of 0.413: sources largely concur on which molecules deserve a word and differ on how readily they apply it. Of 109 descriptors with an estimable effect, 36 show large differential functioning on the ETS scale.
  Against a human panel's reliability, Morgan fingerprints with the full RDKit descriptor block reach 32.9\% of achievable; adding every label from two merged corpora reaches 33.9\%. The gap does not close with model capacity, encoding choice, more molecules, or more words.
  The missing variance is valence. One pleasantness rating per molecule reaches 54.6\% of achievable (57.1\% on an independent older instrument). Valence recovered from descriptors ($ρ= 0.457$) yields only 16.6\%, so it must be measured. Five raters exceed structure plus the full descriptor record; fifteen to twenty saturate. We release a descriptor crosswalk and twenty machine-checked theorems.

</details>


### [262] [Large Distant Gradients Need Not Be Reliable: reliability-weighted credit assignment for long-horizon autoregressive forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12890)
*Junhao Zhao,David Michael Simberg,Jacob Kang,Colin Connor Kurniawan,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Internal Dual-Wiener routing (Internal-DW)，一种仅在反向传播阶段介入的方法，通过可靠性加权内部梯度路径，在保留完整前向长程预测监督的同时抑制不可预测噪声，从而改善自回归长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归预测中，长程 rollout 提供远距离监督，但 BPTT 会让远距离损失的梯度反复经过多层雅可比连乘。这样会使远距离梯度主导更新，同时放大可预测信号与不可预测噪声；因此大梯度未必代表可靠的学习信号，需要在不放弃长程监督的前提下对反向梯度进行可靠性加权。

Method: 提出 Internal Dual-Wiener routing (Internal-DW)：保持完整前向 rollout 和全部 horizon 损失不变，只在反向传播中干预。对每个残差块，为恒等路径与非线性路径推导有界 Wiener 增益，在保留可预测学习信号与抑制不可预测变化之间取得平衡，并利用路径级梯度统计与显式噪声模型估计这些增益。另在已知梯度 SNR 的受控系统中分析远距离梯度增长与其 SNR 下降的关系。

Result: 在受控系统中，远距离梯度会增大但其 SNR 反而下降；Internal-DW 能降低恢复可预测梯度信号的留出误差并改善预测。在四个历史主导、弱驱动测试集上，相较完整 BPTT 预测误差降低 5.2%-13.8%，在四个测试集上均优于梯度裁剪和雅可比正则化，在其中三个上优于验证集选择的截断 BPTT (TBPTT)，并扩展或保持拟合的最优训练 horizon 范围。在全基准套件上存在明确适用边界：当可用历史有限，或所选采样器无法表征主导的驱动相关变化时，收益会减弱甚至反转。

Conclusion: 保留长程监督并不需要同等信任所有反向传播贡献。Internal-DW 通过在反向阶段对内部梯度路径做可靠性加权，在历史主导场景下有效改善长期预测，但其有效性依赖于足够的可用历史以及能代表主导驱动变化的采样器。

Abstract: In autoregressive forecasting, long prediction rollouts provide distant supervision, but backpropagation through time (BPTT) carries gradients from those losses through many autoregressive steps. Repeated Jacobian products can make distant gradients dominate the update while amplifying predictable signal and unpredictable noise together; a large distant gradient therefore need not carry reliable learning signal. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Internal Dual-Wiener routing (Internal-DW), a principled backward-only intervention that preserves the full forward rollout and all horizon losses while reliability-weighting internal gradient routes. At each residual block, we derive bounded Wiener gains for the identity and nonlinear routes that balance preserving predictable learning signal against suppressing unpredictable variation, and estimate them from route-level gradient statistics and an explicit noise model. In a controlled system with known gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that distant gradients can grow even as their SNR falls, and that Internal-DW reduces held-out error in recovering predictable gradient signals and improves forecasting. On four history-dominated, weak-drive testbeds, Internal-DW reduces forecast error by 5.2%-13.8% relative to full BPTT, outperforms gradient clipping and Jacobian regularization on all four, and outperforms validation-selected truncated BPTT (TBPTT) on three. It also extends or preserves the fitted optimal training-horizon range across these four testbeds. Across the full benchmark suite, the current Internal-DW estimator has a clear applicability boundary: its benefit diminishes or reverses when usable history is limited or when the selected sampler fails to represent dominant drive-dependent variation. The results show that retaining long-horizon supervision does not require trusting every backward contribution equally.

</details>


### [263] [Quantifying the Value of Privileged Information Using a PAC-Bayesian Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12891)
*Vasily Bokov,Sebastian Schmitt,Vedran Dunjko,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于PAC-Bayes的信息论框架，用于衡量特权信息（LUPI）的潜在价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUPI中特权信息只在训练时可用，其知识迁移机制缺乏通用理解；Vapnik原有理论等结果依赖特定算法与证明方式，缺少算法无关的通用框架来解释PI何时、如何有用。

Method: 提出算法无关、基于信息论的PAC-Bayes方法：比较有/无PI时最紧可达风险界，定义"潜力"为上界增益；并设计直接从经验训练风险计算该潜力的指标，无需测试时数据访问。

Result: 在监督与无监督设置中验证：训练时指标与真实测试时性能增益之间具有稳健的一致性。

Conclusion: 为LUPI的信息论理解迈出必要一步，可在确定模型前先量化特权特征潜力。该工作属于其他类别（Other）。

Abstract: In practice, various learning scenarios provide access to auxiliary features exclusively during training. Incorporating such data to enhance model performance gave rise to a paradigm known as Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI). While this extra information is intended to improve the resulting model, establishing a generalized, cohesive understanding of how privileged information (PI) transfers useful knowledge remains a challenge. Vapnik's original theory and subsequent works offer performance guarantees in certain cases, but these results are inherently per-algorithm and rely on setting-specific proof approaches. Consequently, a more general framework explaining how and when PI transfers useful knowledge is still missing. To bridge this gap, we introduce an algorithm-agnostic, information-theoretic approach based on the PAC-Bayes framework. Rather than asking whether a particular algorithm exploits PI, we ask how much value it could offer: comparing the tightest achievable risk bound with and without PI yields its potential - an upper limit on the extractable gain. We introduce a metric that quantifies this potential directly from empirical training risk, bypassing the need for test-time data access, and validate our findings in both supervised and unsupervised settings. The results demonstrate a robust correspondence between our training-time metric and true test-time performance gains. Ultimately, this work takes a necessary step toward an information-theoretic understanding of LUPI, and quantifying the potential of privileged features before committing to a model.

</details>


### [264] [Behavior Quotient Learning for Low-Rank Adaptation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12896)
*Pengyang Zhou,Xiaobin Tu,Zhengxi Liu,Rongkun Xue,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Ziyuan Chen,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BQ-LoRA，一种用于 LLM 智能体的单 LoRA 低秩适配框架，通过行为商流形组织轨迹更新，缓解冗余更新与秩预算超限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体需要异构交互能力完成复杂任务。现有多 LoRA 方案会增加适配器存储需求并带来推理时的路由开销；而单 LoRA 虽避免该开销，但在固定秩预算下从多样化的智能体轨迹中学习面临两大挑战：一是不同交互轨迹与参数梯度可能引致等价的决策分布变化，导致重复更新过度强调冗余行为变化；二是聚合更新可能超出适配器秩预算，在权重空间近似会扭曲其本应产生的决策变化。

Method: 提出 BQ-LoRA 低秩适配框架，通过局部行为商流形（local behavior quotient manifold）组织轨迹更新，包含两个模块：行为商平衡（BQB）与决策保持压缩（DPC）。BQB 从决策分布构建商流形，并依据轨迹更新方向在商切空间中的局部密度对其重新加权；DPC 将平衡后的梯度投影到内在固定秩切空间，并通过联合控制有效权重误差与决策分布扭曲来对所得目标进行再分解。

Result: 在 AppWorld 与 BrowseComp-Plus 上与标准 LoRA 及近期低秩适配方法进行对比实验，并通过独立消融实验评估两个模块的互补贡献。

Conclusion: BQ-LoRA 在单一 LoRA 框架下兼顾了低存储与无路由开销的优势，同时通过行为商平衡与决策保持压缩应对多轨迹学习中的冗余更新与秩预算约束问题，为 LLM 智能体的异构交互能力适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-based agents rely on heterogeneous interaction capabilities to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches often distribute these capabilities across multiple LoRA adapters, which increases adapter storage requirements and introduces routing overhead during inference. A single LoRA avoids this overhead, but learning from diverse agent trajectories under a fixed rank budget presents two challenges. First, trajectories with different interaction traces and parameter gradients can induce equivalent changes in decision distributions, causing repeated updates to overemphasize redundant behavioral changes. Second, an aggregated update may exceed the rank budget of the adapter, and approximating it in weight space can distort the decision changes that it is intended to produce. We propose BQ-LoRA, a low-rank adaptation framework that organizes trajectory updates through a local behavior quotient manifold. It contains two modules, i.e., behavior quotient balancing (BQB) and decision preserving compression (DPC). BQB constructs the quotient manifold from decision distributions and reweights trajectory update directions according to their local density in the quotient tangent space. DPC projects the balanced gradient onto the intrinsic fixed rank tangent space and refactorizes the resulting target by jointly controlling effective weight error and distortion of decision distributions. Experiments on AppWorld and BrowseComp-Plus compare BQ-LoRA with standard LoRA and recent low-rank adaptation methods, while separate ablations evaluate the complementary contributions of both components.

</details>


### [265] [Physical-State-Guided Diffusion Sampling for Full-Waveform Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12899)
*Chen Min,Haowen Jiang,Zheng Ma,Xiongbin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理状态引导的扩散采样方法（PSG），通过高斯桥将持续更新的物理速度与扩散先验耦合，实现稳定且高精度的全波形反演（FWI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演（FWI）存在病态性与强非线性问题，其重建精度高度依赖初始化与先验信息。尽管扩散后验采样可提供学习到的地质先验，但将去噪器直接与非线性波动方程求解器耦合，容易产生不可靠的物理引导。因此需要一种既能利用扩散先验、又能保证物理引导可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出物理状态引导的扩散采样（PSG）：通过高斯桥将一个持续维护的物理速度状态与扩散先验相耦合。物理状态由波形拟合进行精化，并用去噪后的速度做正则化；反过来，该物理状态又引导反向扩散过程。该形式化方法将波动方程梯度与去噪器梯度分离，同时保留传统 FWI 的初始化与累积优化历史。

Result: 在四个 OpenFWI 数据族上，PSG 的终端去噪估计在干净与缺失道采集条件下均优于经典方法与基于扩散的基线，并在测量噪声下保持较强的结构恢复能力。多次随机运行可保留主要地质结构，集成变异性集中于地质界面附近，且与局部反演误差正相关。冻结的 OpenFWI 训练先验还能在无需重新训练的情况下支持更大的 Marmousi、Overthrust 和 BP2004 Salt 模型反演，恢复出复杂地质结构。

Conclusion: PSG 通过分离波动方程与去噪器梯度并保留传统 FWI 的优化历史，有效解决了扩散先验与非线性波动求解耦合不可靠的问题，在多个数据集与噪声条件下均表现优异，并具备跨尺度模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) estimates subsurface velocity from seismic recordings, but its ill-posedness and nonlinearity make accurate reconstruction strongly dependent on initialization and prior information. Diffusion posterior sampling provides a learned geological prior, yet directly coupling its denoiser to the nonlinear wave solver can yield unreliable physical guidance. We propose Physical-State-Guided Diffusion Sampling (PSG), which couples a persistent physical velocity to the diffusion prior through a Gaussian bridge. The physical state is refined by waveform fitting regularized by the denoised velocity, and in turn guides the reverse diffusion process. This formulation separates the wave-equation and denoiser gradients while preserving conventional FWI initialization and accumulated optimization history. On four OpenFWI families, PSG's terminal denoised estimates outperform classical and diffusion-based baselines under clean and missing-trace acquisitions and maintain strong structural recovery under measurement noise. Repeated stochastic runs preserve the dominant geological structures, with ensemble variability concentrated near geological interfaces and positively associated with local inversion error. A frozen OpenFWI-trained prior further supports inversion of the larger Marmousi, Overthrust, and BP2004 Salt models, recovering complex geological structures without retraining.

</details>


### [266] [Hidden in Rounds: Predicting the Time Cost of 802.11 Contention in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12903)
*Satwat Bashir,Tasos Dagiuklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用ns-3测量IEEE 802.11信道帧投递率与饱和吞吐量，并将其作为FedAvg更新准入概率的一阶代理来估算通信时间，在720次运行中评估客户端密度与负载对训练轮数和通信时间的影响，并比较均匀参与与异构持久参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在IEEE 802.11无线信道上共享信道，客户端的模型更新传输受信道竞争影响，而客户端密度与负载如何影响训练轮数和通信时间尚缺乏结合链路层测量与FedAvg训练的实证分析。

Method: 使用ns-3测量不同客户端密度与提供负载下的帧投递率和饱和吞吐量；独立FedAvg训练器将帧投递率作为更新准入概率的一阶代理，并用方程估算通信时间；开展720次运行，覆盖两个数据集、两种数据划分、六种客户端密度、六种提供负载和五个随机种子；引入Bianchi锚定的估计器，并比较均匀参与与持久异构参与。

Result: 所有运行均在预设轮数预算内达到目标精度；达到目标所需轮数随提供负载变化很小，而达到目标的通信时间随客户端密度范围增加约两个数量级；Bianchi锚定估计器在留出配置上的平均绝对百分比误差为2.3%至10.2%；未检测到统计上可区分的被排除类精度差距，但置信区间较宽。

Conclusion: 该方法以帧投递率近似更新准入概率，不模拟完整模型更新的传输，也未测量端到端训练时间；估计误差是相对于同一轮时长方程构造的通信时间而非独立测量值；结论仅适用于所评估的配置，不提供一般性的收敛性或公平性保证。

Abstract: Federated learning over IEEE~802.11 shares the wireless channel among clients that send model updates. We use ns-3 to measure the frame-delivery ratio and saturation throughput for different client densities and offered loads. A separate FedAvg trainer uses the frame-delivery ratio as a first-order proxy for the update-admission probability and uses an equation to estimate communication time. The method does not simulate the delivery of a complete model update or measure end-to-end training time. Across 720 evaluated runs with two datasets, two data partitions, six client densities, six offered loads, and five seeds, all runs reached their predefined target accuracy within the round budget. Rounds-to-target changed little with offered load, while communication time-to-target increased by about two orders of magnitude across the client-density range. A Bianchi-anchored estimator produced a mean absolute percentage error from $2.3\%$ to $10.2\%$ on held-out configurations. This error is measured against communication time constructed from the same round-duration equation, not against independently measured completion time. We also compare uniform participation with persistent heterogeneous participation. The study does not detect a statistically distinguishable excluded-class accuracy gap over five seeds, but the confidence intervals are wide. The results apply only to the evaluated configurations and do not provide a general convergence or fairness guarantee.

</details>


### [267] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Wind Farm Control: A Wind Tunnel Study under Dynamic Wind Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12905)
*Yuhan Su,Hongyang Dong,Simone Tamaro,Filippo Campagnolo,Carlo L. Bottasso,Xiaowei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出离线强化学习算法MTD3-BC，用于风向变化下的风电场偏航控制，实现约10%功率增益并通过风洞实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风向变化下风电场功率最大化问题需要偏航控制，但在线强化学习需大量与仿真器交互，计算成本高、训练时间长；且缺乏无需尾流模型的实用控制策略。

Method: 提出无模型的MTD3-BC算法（改进的带行为克隆的双延迟深度确定性策略梯度），为离线强化学习方法，仅利用预先采集的离线数据集推断良好行为；在策略优化目标中引入新的动作一致性项，以保证偏航调整平滑适度。

Result: 风洞实验表明，MTD3-BC有效缓解尾流效应，相比贪婪基线策略获得约10%的场级功率增益，性能与基于数据校准模型的尾流转向基准相当，且无需尾流模型，训练成本仅为在线强化学习的一小部分。

Conclusion: 首次对离线强化学习风电场控制策略进行实验验证与演示，证明其在低训练成本下实现有效偏航控制与功率提升。

Abstract: This paper addresses the wind farm power maximization problem in the presence of wind direction changes. Specifically, a model-free Modified Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient with Behavior Cloning (MTD3-BC) algorithm is proposed to tackle this task through yaw control under varying wind direction conditions. MTD3-BC is an offline reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm that aims to infer good behavior from only a precollected offline dataset. Additionally, to ensure smooth and moderate yaw adjustments, a new action consistency term is introduced into the policy optimization objective. Unlike online RL methods, MTD3-BC does not require extensive interactions with a wind farm simulator during training, significantly reducing computational costs and training time. A wind tunnel experiment is conducted to validate the effectiveness of the algorithm under varying wind directions. The results demonstrate that MTD3-BC successfully mitigates wake effects, delivering farm-level power gains of approximately 10\% over the baseline greedy strategy and performance on par with a data-calibrated model-based wake-steering benchmark, while requiring no wake model and only a small fraction of the training cost of online RL. To our knowledge, this is the first time an offline RL wind farm control policy has been validated and demonstrated experimentally.

</details>


### [268] [A Large-Scale AIS Dataset from Finnish Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12938)
*Debayan Bhattacharya,Ikram Ul Haq,Carlos Pichardo Vicencio,Sebastien Lafond*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个涵盖芬兰水域（波罗的海地区）的AIS数据集，包含2.29亿条数据点，并整理了现有AIS数据集，为海事研究提供资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIS数据最初用于防碰撞，现已扩展到海事多个领域，但缺乏针对波罗的海这一重要区域（含洲际货运、军事活动、冰封水域）的系统性数据集，尤其是包含芬兰湖泊数据的数据资源稀缺。

Method: 收集并整理来自波罗的海及芬兰湖泊的AIS数据，共2.29亿条数据点；对现有AIS数据集进行梳理和分类；详细列出船舶类型及相关特征，并提供海上交通模式的可视化。

Result: 构建了一个大规模AIS数据集（2.29亿数据点），涵盖芬兰水域特色区域，含芬兰湖泊数据；提供了船舶类型与特征清单以及交通模式可视化结果。

Conclusion: 公开发布该AIS数据集旨在推动海事研究的创新与协作，为深入理解波罗的海及芬兰湖泊的海事活动提供入口。

Abstract: This research paper contributes to the maritime research community by introducing a comprehensive AIS dataset from Finnish waters, specifically the Baltic Sea region. AIS data, initially designed for collision prevention, have evolved into a versatile tool with applications across diverse maritime domains. Our paper not only curates and categorises existing AIS datasets but also introduces a collected AIS dataset from the Baltic Sea area, renowned for its intercontinental cargo routes, military activities, and frozen water expanses. This dataset includes 229 millions data points and provides researchers with a resource for studying maritime activities and vessel behaviour in this dynamic region, notably distinguished by the inclusion of data from Finnish lakes. Our analysis includes a detailed listing of ship types and relevant features, empowering researchers to explore various maritime domains. To enhance comprehension and analysis, we provide visualisations of maritime traffic patterns. By publishing this AIS dataset, we aim to catalyse innovation and collaboration in maritime research, offering a gateway to deeper insights into maritime activities in the Baltic Sea and Finnish lakes.

</details>


### [269] [Information-Induced Training Geometry: Exact Reduction, Canonical Completion, and Structured Expressivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12991)
*Zavier Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在仿射不变黎曼几何下，训练数据（通过信息通道可见的余向量）如何约束优化器几何，给出了正定压缩的唯一显式补全、规范不变秩分层与精确降维刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据只能通过一个声明好的信息通道提供部分信息（可见余向量），这种部分信息如何决定相对于参考度量的完整正定余度量、以及哪些自由度无法被识别，是理解优化器几何可辨识性的核心问题。

Method: 在有限维仿射不变黎曼框架下研究满列秩正定压缩：将压缩映射刻画为分裂-Hadamard度量子等距，构造唯一显式补全；用度量定理把球子等距、可达纤维距离与单调径向可见决策问题的无损降维等价起来；并给出光滑分裂-Hadamard定理、闭式收缩、对偶/块优化器族的相对内部锥像检验与确定性/有限样本界。

Result: 得到可见目标的仿射不变最近满几何唯一补全与精确满-可见变分降维；通道变化时补全构成正定锥的规范不变秩分层；闭式拉回配对度量将可见度量运动与子空间旋转分离并给出半正定多方向 Gram 矩阵与参考值模态奇异性；给出闭式先验-数据收缩及对角/块族的锥像检验与恢复误差界。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了所述有限维仿射不变模型中的精确降维、依赖参考的补全与结构化表达能力，为部分可观测信息下优化器几何的可识别性与降维提供了统一理论。

Abstract: Training data constrains optimizer geometry through the covectors visible to a declared information channel. We study how such partial information determines a full positive cometric relative to a reference and which degrees of freedom remain unidentified. Our central result resolves full-column-rank positive-definite compression under affine-invariant Riemannian geometry. The compression map is a split-Hadamard metric submetry and admits an explicit unique completion that is the affine-invariant nearest full geometry realizing a visible target and yields exact full-to-visible variational reduction. When the channel moves, the completions form a gauge-invariant rank stratification of the positive-definite cone. Its closed-form pullback pair metric separates visible-metric motion from subspace rotation through a reference-mismatch weight, yields an explicit positive-semidefinite multi-direction Gram matrix, and exposes the precise singularity of reference-valued modes. The mechanism is explained by a metric theorem equating ball submetry, attained fiber distance, and lossless reduction of every monotone radial visible decision problem. A smooth split-Hadamard theorem supplies coherent information sheets, proximal commutation, and solution-wise gradient-flow lifting. The positive-definite realization also gives closed-form prior-data shrinkage. Diagonal and block optimizer families reduce to relative-interior conic image tests with valid facial certificates, while deterministic and finite-sample bounds quantify recovery of the visible geometry and its subspace. Together these results characterize exact reduction, reference-dependent completion, and structured expressivity for the stated finite-dimensional affine-invariant model.

</details>


### [270] [Dimension-Corrected Hitting Times for Heavy-Tailed Spectral Emergence in Neural Optimizer Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12994)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经网络权重矩阵重尾谱密度的出现时间建模为右删失命中时间问题，提出并验证带维度修正的谱间隙定律，并给出相应的条件性理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾经验谱密度被广泛用作隐式自正则化的诊断工具，但重尾何时出现（出现步数的复杂度）仍缺乏理解，现有研究多关注重尾是否存在而非其形成时间。

Method: 将谱重尾形成形式化为右删失命中时间问题，未在观测期内达到重尾诊断的运行视为删失而非丢弃；在全批量师生动力学中开展受控实验（330次完成的运行），进行有限命中时间回归与右删失对数正态加速失效时间模型拟合；理论上证明线性网络中精确早期损失动力学不能决定因子谱尾、仅Adam递推不蕴含谱重分布、投影奇异基扩散蕴含谱尾势收缩及维度修正的命中时间上界。

Result: 有限命中时间回归支持维度修正谱间隙定律τ_HT≈CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ，R^2=0.683，γ=0.626，ρ=0.772；右删失对数正态AFT模型优于仅含间隙模型，AIC从706.62降至628.70；投影核剖面支持充分扩散机制，Adam与AdamW在测试网格下一致，GD与signGD在同区间未达命中；预训练Qwen2.5-0.5B与Pythia-70M权重相对匹配高斯零假设呈非高斯谱尾结构。

Conclusion: 本文给出了可复现的谱命中时间定律与严格的条件性理论，但不主张Adam必然从第一性原理生成重尾。

Abstract: Heavy-tailed empirical spectral densities of neural-network weight matrices are widely used as diagnostics of implicit self-regularization, but the step complexity of heavy-tail emergence remains poorly understood. We formulate spectral heavy-tail formation as a right-censored hitting-time problem: a run that does not reach a heavy-tail diagnostic within the observation horizon is treated as censored rather than discarded. In controlled full-batch teacher--student dynamics, we find that the first-step spike--bulk gap alone does not explain onset time. Instead, finite-onset regression supports a dimension-corrected spectral-gap law, (τ_{\mathrm{HT}}\approx CΔ_1^{-γ}d^ρ), with (R^2=0.683), (γ=0.626), and (ρ=0.772) across 330 completed runs. Right-censored lognormal accelerated-failure-time models further favor the dimension-corrected model over a gap-only model, improving AIC from 706.62 to 628.70. Theoretically, we prove that exact early loss dynamics in linear networks do not determine factor spectral tails, that Adam recurrences alone do not imply spectral redistribution, and that projected singular-basis spreading implies contraction of a spectral-tail potential and hence a dimension-corrected hitting-time bound. Empirically, projected-kernel profiles support the sufficient spreading mechanism, Adam and AdamW agree under tested grids, GD and signGD do not reach onset in the same regimes, and real pretrained Qwen2.5-0.5B and Pythia-70M transformer weights show non-Gaussian spectral-tail structure relative to matched Gaussian nulls. The result is a reproducible spectral hitting-time law with rigorous conditional theory, not a claim that Adam necessarily generates heavy tails from first principles.

</details>


### [271] [A Full Adam Theorem for Spectral Heavy-Tail Onset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12996)
*Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在封闭高斯Stein-Hermite师生状态演化模型中，完整证明了Adam优化器的谱重尾发生定理，给出了依赖于首个尖峰-体谱间隙的命中时间定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为Adam优化器在重尾谱动力学中的"谱重尾发生"现象提供完整的理论证明，此前相关工作多依赖简化假设，未能从实际Adam递推出发严格推导谱命中定律。同时希望厘清任意梯度下更强定理是否可能，以及二阶线性网络损失动力学能否识别因子谱。

Method: 依托封闭高斯Stein-Hermite师生状态演化模型：从完整批量Adam递推出发，用Stein-Hermite演算推导总体梯度；证明有限宽度协方差集中；将多步Adam动量转化为精确非中心高斯符号核；用基均一化定理控制对角Adam分母；由Hermite边缘转移定理导出正则变化投影更新响应；经精确Gram更新传递响应；最终证明带匹配上下命中界的近似目标KL收缩。

Result: 得到最终谱命中定律τ_ε=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ}d^ρ log(Ψ_0/ε))，其中Δ_1为第一个尖峰-体谱间隙。"完整"体现在从Adam动量与分母到谱命中定律的每一步均在封闭状态演化模型中形式化。此外证明两项否定性结论：更强的任意梯度Adam定理不可能成立；精确两步线性网络损失动力学无法识别因子谱或重尾命中时间。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Adam在谱重尾发生上的完整定理，明确了命中时间与谱间隙的定量关系，并划定了理论的适用边界——任意梯度下的更强结论不可得，二阶线性网络动力学亦不足以识别谱信息。

Abstract: We prove a full Adam theorem for spectral heavy-tail onset in a closed Gaussian Stein-Hermite teacher-student state-evolution model. The theorem begins with the actual full-batch Adam recurrences, derives the population gradient by Stein-Hermite calculus, proves finite-width covariance concentration, converts multi-step Adam momentum into an exact non-centered Gaussian sign kernel, controls the diagonal Adam denominator by a basis-homogenization theorem, derives a regularly varying projected update response from a Hermite edge-transfer theorem, pushes the response through the exact Gram update, and proves approximate-target KL contraction with matching upper and lower hitting bounds. The final law is (τ_\varepsilon=Θ(Δ_1^{-γ}d^ρ\log(Ψ_0/\varepsilon))), where (Δ_1) is the first spike-bulk spectral gap. The result is full in the following precise sense: every step from Adam's momentum and denominator to the spectral hitting law is formalized inside the closed state-evolution model. We also prove that a stronger arbitrary-gradient Adam theorem is impossible, and that exact two-step linear-network loss dynamics do not identify factor spectra or heavy-tail hitting times.

</details>


### [272] [Dual-guided Hierarchical Edge Localization for Large-scale Optimal Transport Across Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13010)
*Wenzhou Xia,Qiaoqiao Ding,Jingwei Liang,Xiaoqun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELLO：将大规模离散最优传输转化为边定位问题的分层求解器，利用对偶势引导由粗到细初始化与层内精炼，实现线性内存与百万级规模加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无正则化的离散最优传输需要求解含二次方数量传输变量的线性规划，计算与内存开销巨大，难以扩展到百万级样本规模。论文旨在设计可扩展的离散OT求解器。

Method: 提出HELLO分层求解器，把大规模离散OT视为边定位（edge localization）。用对偶势指导：一是通过递归子采样层级传播粗粒度对偶势，用于由粗到细的初始化并指派候选边；二是层内精炼，按行与列迭代插入最大对偶违反者，直到相对KKT残差满足给定容差，并用预算化剪枝保证线性内存。对于精确算术精炼，在符号字典序规则下证明有限步终止于全局最优。

Result: 在百万点规模上，跨特征维度从个位数到数千维，HELLO相较强基线获得更低的传输目标值与数量级的运行时间提升；单张H100上可扩展到每个边缘分布128万样本、8192维，峰值GPU内存41.6 GiB，且满足低于10^-6的完整相对KKT残差。

Conclusion: HELLO是高效可扩展的大规模离散OT求解框架，支持一般成对代价，并可作为可扩展的平衡OT oracle，服务于半离散OT、Gromov–Wasserstein、非平衡OT以及基于OT的流匹配。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) compares distributions and aligns datasets in machine learning, yet unregularized discrete OT requires a linear program with quadratically many transport variables. We propose HELLO, a hierarchical solver that casts large-scale discrete OT as edge localization and uses dual potentials to guide both coarse-to-fine initialization and within-level refinement. Initialization propagates coarse dual potentials across a recursive subsampling hierarchy to assign candidate edges. Refinement then iteratively inserts the largest dual violators in each row and column until the relative KKT residual meets a prescribed tolerance, while budgeted pruning ensures linear memory complexity. For exact-arithmetic refinement, we prove finite termination at a global optimum under a symbolic lexicographic rule. At the million-point scale, HELLO attains lower transport objectives with order-of-magnitude runtime improvements over strong baselines across feature dimensions from single digits to thousands. It further scales to 1.28 million samples per marginal in 8192 dimensions on a single H100, using 41.6 GiB peak GPU memory while satisfying a full relative KKT residual below $10^{-6}$. Beyond standard discrete OT, the framework supports general pairwise costs and serves as a scalable balanced-OT oracle for semi-discrete OT, Gromov--Wasserstein, unbalanced OT, and OT-based Flow Matching.

</details>


### [273] [Attention Quantization for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13031)
*Jonas M. Kübler,Benjamin Jäger,Klemens Flöge,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对表格基础模型（TabPFN-v3、TabICLv2）的推理效率，提出了一种面向注意力计算的FP8量化策略与Triton内核，实现最高1.7倍加速且精度无明显下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着表格基础模型的兴起，其推理性能优化成为新兴的效率研究方向。尽管这类模型在架构上与基于Transformer的大语言模型（LLM）相似，但其模型规模和serving模式存在显著差异，因此LLM中常用的权重或KV缓存量化并不适用，优化重点应放在注意力计算上。

Method: 作者开发了一种针对query、key、value的FP8量化策略，并使用显式FP8矩阵乘法指令来加速注意力计算；同时发现必须让测试行的量化误差与训练行的量化误差对齐，否则精度会急剧下降；最终通过Triton内核实现加速。

Result: Triton内核相比常规16位内核实现了最高1.7倍的加速，并且在TabPFN-v3和TabICLv2上，于TabArena和BeyondArena基准中均无相关精度损失。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的推理优化应聚焦于注意力计算而非权重/KV缓存量化，通过精心设计的FP8量化与误差对齐策略，可在保证精度的前提下显著提升推理速度。

Abstract: With the recent rise and adoption of tabular foundation models, optimizing their inference performance becomes an emerging field for efficiency research. While the models are architecturally similar to transformer-based large language models (LLMs), the size and serving patterns differ significantly. We show that the focus should be on the attention calculation and less on weight or KV cache quantization, which are more popular in LLMs. We develop a quantization strategy for queries, keys, and values to FP8 and use explicit FP8 matrix multiplication instructions to speed up the attention calculation. We find that it is crucial to align the quantization error in the test rows with the quantization error in the training rows, as otherwise the accuracy drops drastically. Our Triton kernel achieves a speedup up to 1.7x over regular 16-bit kernels, and we show that on TabPFN-v3 and TabICLv2 there is no relevant accuracy loss across TabArena and BeyondArena.

</details>


### [274] [Groupoid-Based Internal State Representations for Reinforcement Learning with Local Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13035)
*Ben Opperman,Eduardo Alonso,Esther Mondragón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于广群（groupoid）的强化学习框架，动态捕捉局部、状态依赖的对称性以提升样本效率与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法多依赖固定群作用或预定义状态抽象，经典算法假设全局结构化的MDP与统一适用的动作和转移，难以利用真实环境中的模块化与局部、上下文相关的规律性。

Method: 使用广群刻画局部、状态依赖的对称性，支持交互过程中动态发现等价结构；智能体维护轨道代表元（orbit representatives）及将原始状态映射到规范形式的传输子（transporters），在对称性约简的空间中学习与决策，同时保留局部区分。

Result: 在具有强部分对称性的密集、大规模环境中，该方法提升了样本效率与收敛速度，相较标准Q-learning取得显著性能增益。

Conclusion: 动态利用局部对称性为可扩展、可泛化的强化学习提供了一条实用且具数学原理支撑的路径。

Abstract: Symmetries play a central role in reducing the complexity of reinforcement learning problems, yet most existing approaches rely on fixed group actions or predefined state abstractions. Classical reinforcement learning algorithms typically assume a globally structured Markov decision process with uniformly applicable actions and transitions, an assumption that limits their ability to exploit modularity and local, context-dependent regularities present in many realistic environments. We propose a reinforcement learning framework using groupoids to capture local, state-dependent symmetries and support the dy- namic discovery of equivalence structures during interaction. The agent maintains orbit representatives together with transporters that map raw states to canonical forms, enabling learning and decision-making to be performed in a symmetry-reduced space while preserving local distinctions. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed groupoid-based approach improves sample efficiency and convergence in dense and large-scale environments exhibiting strong partial symmetries, yielding substantial performance gains over standard Q-learning. These findings show that dynamically exploiting local symmetry provides a practical and mathematically principled route to scalable and generalisable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [275] [Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13039)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Jacopo Bono,Hugo Ferreira,Pedro Ribeiro,Pedro Saleiro,Pedro Bizarro,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"演化域迁移学习(TrED)"这一新问题形式,将传统迁移学习子领域统一为部署系统随时间经历的一条轨迹,并指出该问题尚未被解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习研究被分割为域泛化、域适应、多域学习等孤立子领域,各自对目标数据的可用性(数据量与标签量)做出不同假设。但现实应用中数据可用性并非固定不变,而是随时间演化——实例与标签从新域中被逐步收集。因此每个经典设定只描述了部署系统必须完整经历的一条轨迹中的一个快照,缺乏对整条轨迹的统一刻画。

Method: 作者将这一轨迹本身形式化为一个新的迁移学习问题,称为"面向演化域的迁移学习(TrED)"。该形式化由三要素构成:由环境固定的数据可用性过程、方法可自由选择的学习协议、以及评估整条模型轨迹(而非单一模型)的评价准则。在此框架下,经典设定被重新解释为学习器可能经历的若干"状态区段",而非彼此独立的问题。随后作者对迁移学习文献进行梳理,识别有潜力作为解决方案组件的机制。

Result: 文献调研发现:大多数方法仅针对单一状态区段(regime)进行定制,即便是最强的现有候选方法也未能优化整条轨迹。因此经典方法无法直接迁移到TrED设定。

Conclusion: TrED是一个定义良好且尚未解决的问题,构成未来研究的重要方向。作者呼吁研究者将迁移学习视为对整条数据可用性轨迹的优化,而非孤立快照的拼接。

Abstract: Transfer learning explores how to leverage knowledge from various tasks or domains (sources) to enhance predictive performance in related tasks or domains (targets). Typically, transfer learning research is segmented into several isolated sub-areas (such as domain generalisation, domain adaptation, or multi-domain learning), each making distinct assumptions about target data availability, namely how much data and how many labels are available at training time. However, in many real-world applications, data availability is not fixed but evolves over time, as instances and labels are progressively collected from a new domain. Each of the classical settings then describes only a snapshot of a trajectory that a deployed system must traverse in full. We formalise this trajectory as a transfer learning problem in its own right, Transfer Learning for Evolving Domains (TrED), specified by a data availability process fixed by the environment, a learning protocol that the method is free to choose, and an evaluation criterion that scores the whole trajectory of models rather than a single one. Within this formalism, the classical settings are recovered as regimes that a learner may pass through, rather than as separate problems that TrED concatenates. We then examine the transfer learning literature to identify mechanisms that are promising building blocks for a solution, and find that most methods are tailored to a single regime and that even the strongest existing candidates do not yet optimise the whole trajectory. We argue that TrED is a well-posed and unsolved problem, and an important direction for future research.

</details>


### [276] [Quantile-based Loss Filtering for Outlier-Robust Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13040)
*Jamie Haddock,Anna Ma,Elizaveta Rebrova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种通用的损失过滤框架——分位数-k-损失SGD（QkL-SGD），用于处理有限和优化中部分分量函数被损坏（梯度不可靠）的问题，并证明了线性收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限和优化中部分分量函数可能被损坏，导致梯度高度不可靠。已有的min-k-loss SGD和针对损坏线性系统的分位数方法存在局限（如min-k易反复选择近乎求解的分量而停滞），因此需要一个更通用的损失过滤框架来兼顾鲁棒性与信息量。

Method: 提出QkL-SGD方法：每次迭代采样k个分量损失，并从较低的实测q-分位数中均匀选择一个索引来更新。方法上给出了两类分析：一是标准凸性假设下的线性收敛分析，要求样本量随损坏数量及子集强凸阈值缩放；二是针对样本量k很小或不宜增大的情况，提供覆盖任意样本量k的小样本概率分析，其收敛行为取决于选中离群点的概率与所选好步的曲率。

Result: 证明了该方法的线性收敛性，并给出小样本下的概率收敛刻画。在多项式回归、正则化逻辑回归和正则化hinge损失上的实验表明：中间分位数往往同时优于标准SGD和min-k-loss SGD；min-k常因反复选择近乎求解的分量而停滞，而中间分位数保持鲁棒性并产生更有信息量的更新。

Conclusion: QkL-SGD作为一个通用损失过滤框架，在理论上具备收敛保证，在实验中表明采用中间分位数比极端的分位数（如min-k）更有效，兼顾鲁棒性与更新信息量。

Abstract: We study loss-based filtering for finite-sum optimization with a subset of corrupted component functions whose gradients may be highly unreliable. Motivated by minimum-loss-based SGD (min-$k$-loss) and quantile-based methods for corrupted linear systems, we propose and analyze a general loss-filtering framework -- Quantile-\(k\)-Loss SGD (Q\(k\)L-SGD) -- that samples \(k\) component losses at each iteration and updates using an index chosen uniformly from the lower empirical \(q\)-quantile. We prove linear convergence of this family of methods under standard convexity assumptions, requiring the sample size to scale with the number of corruptions and a subset strong-convexity threshold. For the cases when large enough sampling is impossible or undesirable, we give a complementary small-sample probabilistic analysis that covers any sample size $k$ and the convergence behavior depends on the probability of selecting an outlier and on the curvature of the selected good step. Experiments on polynomial regression, regularized logistic regression, and regularized hinge loss show that intermediate quantiles often outperform both standard SGD and min-\(k\)-loss SGD. In particular, min-\(k\) often stalls by repeatedly selecting nearly solved components, while intermediate quantiles retain robustness and produce more informative updates.

</details>


### [277] [DynSHAP: Towards Explainable Dynamic Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13042)
*Nastasya Anokhina,Jonas Jürß,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DynSHAP框架，将SHAP扩展至动态生存分析，处理纵向不规则输入与函数型生存输出，并引入Temporal DynSHAP学习特征时间依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态生存分析（DSA）深度学习模型虽预测性能强，但黑盒特性限制临床信任与采用；现有可解释性方法无法同时处理纵向、不规则输入与函数型生存输出。

Method: DynSHAP将时间-特征对视为Shapley博弈中的参与者，把常规边际SHAP估计器扩展至DSA场景；进一步提出Temporal DynSHAP，学习特征随时间的线性依赖关系，并通过条件采样在解释中处理这些依赖。

Result: 在具有已知真实归因的合成数据上，Temporal DynSHAP比边际估计器更准确地恢复时间依赖特征；在两个真实临床数据集和两种DSA架构上，DynSHAP产生的归因与模型学习结果一致。

Conclusion: DynSHAP使医学专家能够识别哪些患者信息在何时驱动了预测，从而提升DSA模型的可解释性与临床可用性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for dynamic survival analysis (DSA) achieve strong predictive performance by incorporating longitudinal patient data, but their black box nature limits clinical trust and adoption. Existing explainability methods cannot handle longitudinal, irregular inputs and functional survival outputs simultaneously, which limits their usability in DSA. We propose DynSHAP, a SHAP framework suited specifically for dynamic survival analysis. It extends common marginal SHAP estimators to this setting by treating time--feature pairs as players in the Shapley game. We further introduce Temporal DynSHAP, which learns linear dependencies in features over time and uses conditional sampling to address them in explanations. When applied to synthetic data with known ground-truth attributions, Temporal DynSHAP recovers temporally dependent features more accurately than marginal estimators for a given state-of-the-art model. Applied to two real-world clinical datasets and two DSA architectures, DynSHAP produces attributions faithful to model learning, allowing medical experts to see which patient information drove the prediction and when.

</details>


### [278] [Robust Policy Optimization via Adversarial Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13044)
*Amine Andam,Jamal Bentahar,Mustapha Hedabou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对深度强化学习（DRL）鲁棒性研究中算法设计、实现和评估三个阶段的局限，提出对抗重要性采样方法 Advis、模块化库 advrl，并改进评估协议，在连续控制任务上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向输入扰动的鲁棒 DRL 研究在三个阶段均存在不足：算法上难以同时满足无需额外环境交互、无需辅助网络且捕捉长期鲁棒性；实现上缺少统一、可复现的工具支持；评估上依赖有限的攻击者配置，可能高估鲁棒性。因此作者希望系统性解决这些问题。

Method: 作者提出 Adversarial Importance Sampling（Advis），利用标准训练轨迹上的重要性采样来估计并优化可验证的最坏情况回报，无需额外环境交互和辅助网络，并能捕捉长期鲁棒性。同时发布模块化 PyTorch 库 advrl，提供现有鲁棒性方法与对抗攻击的单文件实现。评估方面，重新审视学习型对抗攻击，指出最优对抗超参数无法在不同智能体间迁移，并使用比先前工作多 6-14 倍的攻击者配置进行评估。

Result: 在连续控制环境中，所提方法相对现有基线表现出更好的有效性；同时表明有限攻击者配置会导致鲁棒性被高估。

Conclusion: 该工作从算法、工具和评估三个层面推进了鲁棒 DRL 研究，强调可验证的最坏情况优化与多样化对抗评估的重要性，并公开代码以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in safeguarding deep reinforcement learning (DRL) policies against input perturbations. Developing robust DRL involves three main stages: algorithm design, implementation, and evaluation. In this work, we identify and address a key limitation at each stage. First, we introduce Adversarial Importance Sampling (Advis), a method that uses importance sampling over trajectories from standard training to estimate and optimize verifiable worst-case returns. Advis satisfies three desirable criteria not jointly achieved by prior work: it requires no additional environment interactions, no auxiliary networks, and captures long-term robustness. Second, we introduce advrl, a modular PyTorch library that provides clean, single-file implementations of existing robustness methods and adversarial attacks, facilitating rapid prototyping and enabling reproducible and traceable evaluations. Third, we revisit evaluation under learned adversaries and show that optimal adversarial hyperparameters do not transfer across agents, which can lead to an overestimation of robustness when using a limited set of attacker configurations. Accordingly, we evaluate policies against a large and diverse set of attackers, using 6-14x more configurations than prior work. Finally, we evaluate our approach on continuous control environments, demonstrating its effectiveness relative to existing baselines. The code is available at: https://github.com/AmineAndam04/advrl

</details>


### [279] [MCRL2: Multi-resource Cross-attention-based Representation Learning-augmented Reinforcement Learning for Cloud Microservice Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13048)
*Tiangang Li,Shi Ying,Xiangbo Tian,Chuan Shi,Ding Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCRL2，一种结合多资源交叉注意力表示学习的强化学习方法，用于微服务调度，实验证明其在负载均衡、调度成功率和平均完成时间上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
